📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本周研究揭示了H5N1病毒在北美家禽和奶牛中的广泛传播与适应性突变,H5N5在北极野生动物中的出现,以及新型H3N8/H3N3病毒在中国的潜在风险,同时展示了新型疫苗和抗病毒策略的进展。
  • H5N1病毒在宾夕法尼亚州家禽中至少68次独立引入,并发现哺乳动物适应性突变PB2 D701N [10.64898/2026.07.17.739193]。
  • 牛源H5N1病毒可通过乳腺感染哺乳母猪,且感染呈亚临床状态,存在传播风险 [10.64898/2026.07.18.739312]。
  • H5N5病毒在斯瓦尔巴群岛北极熊和大西洋海象中检出,携带哺乳动物适应标记PB2-E627V [10.64898/2026.07.20.739480]。
  • 中国监测发现新型H3N8和H3N3禽流感病毒,具有哺乳动物适应相关氨基酸替换 [10.1016/j.tvjl.2026.106789]。
  • This week's studies reveal widespread H5N1 transmission and adaptive mutations in North American poultry and dairy cattle, emergence of H5N5 in Arctic wildlife, potential risk of novel H3N8/H3N3 viruses in China, and progress in novel vaccines and antiviral strategies.
  • H5N1 was introduced into Pennsylvania poultry at least 68 times independently, with mammalian adaptive mutation PB2 D701N detected [10.64898/2026.07.17.739193].
  • Bovine-derived H5N1 efficiently infects lactating swine via mammary gland, with subclinical infection posing spillover risk [10.64898/2026.07.18.739312].
  • H5N5 detected in polar bear and walrus in Svalbard, carrying mammalian adaptation marker PB2-E627V [10.64898/2026.07.20.739480].
  • Novel H3N8 and H3N3 avian influenza viruses identified in Chinese poultry, with amino acid substitutions linked to mammalian adaptation [10.1016/j.tvjl.2026.106789].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Analytical and Bioanalytical Chemistry  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-22  |  DOI: 10.1007/s00216-026-06655-3  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

通过 RNA 标准物质的开发和表征支持 A(H5N1) 的分子检测

Supporting molecular detection of A(H5N1) through development and characterization of RNA reference materials
作者： Eileen A. Moran, Monique E. Hunter, Jason G. Kralj, Kristine Gierz, Julia C. Frederick, Jimma Liddell, Lisa M. Keong, Bo Shu, John R. Barnes, Jasmine Chaitram
摘要中文翻译2024 年,美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 和美国国家标准与技术研究所 (NIST) 启动了一项跨机构合作,以加强对自 2021 年以来在北美传播的高致病性 禽流感 A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒造成的大流行情况的准备。多个 跨物种溢出 事件针对哺乳动物物种,包括奶牛中的 暴发 和人类病例的激增,刺激了 RNA 参考材料的开发,以协助新诊断的开发和验证。根据 CDC 的序列输入,NIST 生成了 A(H5N1) 血凝素、神经氨酸酶和基质蛋白 1 和 2 基因片段的三种合成 RNA 材料。在这里,我们描述了利用液滴数字逆转录聚合酶链反应这些参考材料的开发和表征。通过此次合作,我们能够快速向公众提供量化、同质、稳定且与流行病学相关的 RNA 参考材料,以帮助新检测方法的开发和验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究旨在加强对自 2021 年以来在北美流行的 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒引起的潜在大流行的准备,目的是开发 RNA 参考材料以协助新诊断的开发和验证。
设计与对象
这项研究涉及美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 和美国国家标准与技术研究院 (NIST) 之间的合作,开发 A(H5N1) 病毒基因片段的 RNA 参考材料,该研究受到 跨物种溢出 事件的推动,在包括奶牛和人类病例在内的哺乳动物物种中发挥作用。
核心方法
NIST 利用液滴数字逆转录聚合酶链式反应进行表征,生成了 A(H5N1) 血凝素、神经氨酸酶和基质蛋白 1 和 2 基因片段的三种合成 RNA 材料。
主要结果
该研究成功开发并表征了定量、均质、稳定且与流行病学相关的 RNA 标准物质,以帮助新的检测方法开发和验证。
解释与创新
作者将此次合作解释为能够快速提供参考材料,强调了他们的方法在解决 A(H5N1) 暴发 期间对诊断工具的迫切需求方面的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
通过提供用于测定验证的标准化参考材料,这些发现可以为 监测 和 禽流感 暴发 的诊断准备提供信息,特别是在有 A(H5N1) 病毒株流行的地区。
局限与证据强度
尽管 CDC 和 NIST 之间的合作提高了可靠性,但证据强度中等,因为该研究侧重于参考材料的开发,没有临床验证或更广泛的流行病学评估。

Supporting molecular detection of A(H5N1) through development and characterization of RNA reference materials

Authors: Eileen A. Moran, Monique E. Hunter, Jason G. Kralj, Kristine Gierz, Julia C. Frederick, Jimma Liddell, Lisa M. Keong, Bo Shu, John R. Barnes, Jasmine Chaitram
Original abstract (English)
In 2024, the US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) and National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST) initiated an interagency collaboration to bolster preparedness in the event of a pandemic scenario due to high pathogenicity avian influenza A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses that had been circulating in North America since 2021. Multiple spillover events into mammalian species, including an outbreak among dairy cattle and a surge in human cases, spurred the development of RNA reference materials to assist with new diagnostics development and validation. With input from CDC on sequences, NIST generated three synthetic RNA materials of A(H5N1) hemagglutinin, neuraminidase, and matrix proteins 1 and 2 gene segments. Here, we describe the development and characterization of these reference materials utilizing droplet digital reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction. Through this collaboration, we were able to rapidly provide quantified, homogeneous, stable, and epidemiologically relevant RNA reference materials to the public to aid new assay development and validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to bolster preparedness for a potential pandemic caused by highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses circulating in North America since 2021, with the objective of developing RNA reference materials to assist new diagnostics development and validation.
Design and population
The study involved a collaboration between the US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) and the National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST) to develop RNA reference materials for A(H5N1) virus gene segments, spurred by spillover events into mammalian species including dairy cattle and human cases.
Core methods
NIST generated three synthetic RNA materials of A(H5N1) hemagglutinin, neuraminidase, and matrix proteins 1 and 2 gene segments, utilizing droplet digital reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction for characterization.
Main results
The study successfully developed and characterized quantified, homogeneous, stable, and epidemiologically relevant RNA reference materials to aid new assay development and validation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the collaboration as enabling rapid provision of reference materials, highlighting the novelty of their approach in addressing the urgent need for diagnostic tools during the A(H5N1) outbreak.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and diagnostic preparedness for avian influenza outbreaks, particularly in regions with circulating A(H5N1) strains, by providing standardized reference materials for assay validation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study focuses on reference material development without clinical validation or broader epidemiological assessment, though the collaboration between CDC and NIST enhances reliability.

来自宾夕法尼亚州农场和活禽市场的家禽中 宿主 的 宿主 内和 宿主 之间的动态

Within- and between-host dynamics of highly pathogenic avian influenza in domestic birds from Pennsylvania farms and live bird markets
作者： Anna S. Jaeger, Elena Cruz-Adames, Stephen D. Shank, Irina Chupikova, Katie Kumta, Eman Anis, Louise H. Moncla, Jaeger, A. S., Shank, S. D., Moncla, L. H.
摘要中文翻译自 2021 年底以来,H5 亚型分支 2.3.4.4b 的 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAI) 已在美洲蔓延,毁灭了野生动物和农业动物,并导致数十人死亡。国家 监测 策略通常只为每种家禽 暴发 提供一个代表性序列,排除了对 暴发 进化 内的精细地理 传播 推断或研究。我们从 2023 年至 2025 年从宾夕法尼亚州商业农场和活禽市场 (LBM) 暴发 采样的 46 只受感染的鸡形目和雁形目样本中生成了高质量的深度序列数据。我们发现 H5N1 病毒至少被引入宾夕法尼亚州 68 次。我们恢复了同一县内相隔 3 周的活禽市场 暴发 的独立来源,以及宾夕法尼亚州 LBM 和纽约商业鸟类之间的 传播,表明东北部活禽市场分销系统内的 传播 风险较高。对农场内变异群体的分析显示,来自同一 暴发 的样本之间经常存在变异共享,这表明变异在流行病学相关的感染中传播。我们在这些样本中鉴定出了 9 个已知的适应性 突变,其中包括 LBM 鸡样本中的一个 PB2 D701N 实例,这表明虽然罕见,但有关哺乳动物适应性的 突变 可能存在于这些家养 暴发 中。我们的数据表明,家禽 暴发 支持高循环多样性和广泛的 传播 瓶颈,增加了少数变异在感染之间产生和传播的风险。这些数据可以帮助为有针对性的生物安全措施提供信息,并更好地量化农业 暴发 期间病毒适应的风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查家禽中 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 的 宿主 内部和 宿主 之间的动态,解决了解家禽 高致病性禽流感 中病毒传播和 进化 的需要。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2023 年至 2025 年间从宾夕法尼亚州商业农场和活禽市场 (LBM) 暴发 采样的 46 只受感染鸡形目和雁形目动物的深度序列数据。
核心方法
使用高质量深度测序来分析 暴发 内的变异群体,并对来自同一 暴发 和不同位置的样本进行比较,以评估 传播 模式。
主要结果
研究发现,H5N1 病毒至少有 68 次独立引入宾夕法尼亚州,同一县内观察到的 LBM 暴发 的独立起源相隔 3 周。此外,还鉴定出了 9 种已知的适应性 突变,其中包括 LBM 鸡样本中的 PB2 D701N 实例。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,家禽 暴发 支持高循环多样性和广泛的 传播 瓶颈,这可能会增加少数变异在感染之间产生和传播的风险。
科研与公卫意义
这些数据有助于为有针对性的生物安全措施提供信息,并更好地量化农业 暴发 期间病毒适应的风险,这对预防人类 跨物种溢出 和人畜共患 传播 具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对单一地理区域的关注以及病毒多样性的潜在代表性不足而受到限制,但由于使用了深度测序和详细的 暴发 内分析,证据是强有力的。

Within- and between-host dynamics of highly pathogenic avian influenza in domestic birds from Pennsylvania farms and live bird markets

Authors: Anna S. Jaeger, Elena Cruz-Adames, Stephen D. Shank, Irina Chupikova, Katie Kumta, Eman Anis, Louise H. Moncla, Jaeger, A. S., Shank, S. D., Moncla, L. H.
Original abstract (English)
Since late 2021, highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAI) of the H5 subtype clade 2.3.4.4b have spread across the Americas, devastating wildlife, agricultural animals, and resulting in dozens of human spillovers. National surveillance strategies generally provide only a single representative sequence per poultry outbreak, precluding fine-scale geographic transmission inference or studies of within-outbreak evolution. We produced high-quality deep sequence data from 46 infected Galliformes and Anseriformes sampled from commercial farm and live bird market (LBM) outbreaks in Pennsylvania from 2023-2025. We found that H5N1 viruses were introduced into Pennsylvania at least 68 independent times. We recover independent origins of live bird market outbreaks within the same county 3 weeks apart, and transmission between Pennsylvania LBM and New York commercial birds, suggesting high transmission risk within the Northeast live bird market distribution system. Analyses of within-farm variant populations show frequent variant sharing between samples from the same outbreak, suggesting that variants are propagated among epidemiologically linked infections. We identified 9 known adaptive mutations in these samples, including one instance of PB2 D701N in a LBM chicken sample, suggesting that while rare, concerning mammalian adaptive mutations can be present within these domestic outbreaks. Our data suggest that domestic bird outbreaks support high circulating diversity and wide transmission bottlenecks, increasing the risk of minority variants arising and propagating between infections. These data can help inform targeted biosecurity measures and better quantify the risk of viral adaptation during agricultural outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the within- and between-host dynamics of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) in domestic birds, addressing the need to understand viral spread and evolution in poultry outbreaks.
Design and population
The study analyzed deep sequence data from 46 infected Galliformes and Anseriformes sampled from commercial farm and live bird market (LBM) outbreaks in Pennsylvania between 2023 and 2025.
Core methods
High-quality deep sequencing was used to analyze variant populations within outbreaks, with comparisons made between samples from the same outbreak and across different locations to assess transmission patterns.
Main results
The study found that H5N1 viruses were introduced into Pennsylvania at least 68 independent times, with independent origins of LBM outbreaks within the same county observed 3 weeks apart. Additionally, 9 known adaptive mutations were identified, including one instance of PB2 D701N in a LBM chicken sample.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that domestic bird outbreaks support high circulating diversity and wide transmission bottlenecks, which may increase the risk of minority variants arising and propagating between infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
These data can help inform targeted biosecurity measures and better quantify the risk of viral adaptation during agricultural outbreaks, which has implications for preventing human spillovers and zoonotic transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single geographic region and the potential for underrepresentation of viral diversity, but the evidence is strong due to the use of deep sequencing and detailed within-outbreak analysis.

站立 HA 表型宽度形状 H5N1 交叉 宿主 潜力

Standing HA phenotypic breadth shapes H5N1 cross-host potential
作者： Justin Bahl, M H M Mubassir, Sachin Subedi, Tanin Rajamand, Mohamed Bakheet, Ludy Carmola, Sihua Peng, Rajan Kandel, Guppy Stott, Robert J. Woods
摘要中文翻译摘要 将遗传变异与功能表型联系起来仍然是评估新兴病毒的跨 宿主 潜力的主要障碍。在这里,我们重建了北美流行的约 13,000 个 高致病性禽流感 A H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒的预测血凝素 (HA) 表型特征的 进化。禽流感 在北美的广泛地理分布引发了一波广泛的 HA 表型多样性浪潮,后来通过禽类 宿主 的选择性扫描而得到完善。然而,在奶牛中建立后,病毒群体在 HA 中表现出新的表型多样化,包括尽管受体结合结构域高度保守,但对 α2,6 连接的唾液酸苷的参与度增加。这些模式表明,与牛相关的HA表型可以从循环病毒群体内的长期预测的HA表型宽度中得出,同时在与牛相关的循环过程中继续多样化,而不是仅遵循通过规范受体结合替代的简单逐步适应性路径。通过将病毒序列变异与自然进化群体中预测的蛋白质特性联系起来,该框架提供了一种可扩展的策略,用于优先考虑具有跨 宿主 相关特征的 H5N1 变体,以实现目标 监测 和实验随访。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
摘要 将遗传变异与功能表型联系起来仍然是评估新兴病毒的跨 宿主 潜力的主要障碍。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
然而,在奶牛中建立后,病毒群体在 HA 中表现出新的表型多样化,包括尽管受体结合结构域高度保守,但对 α2,6 连接的唾液酸苷的参与度增加。
解释与创新
这些模式表明,与牛相关的HA表型可以从循环病毒群体内的长期预测的HA表型宽度中得出,同时在与牛相关的循环过程中继续多样化,而不是仅遵循通过规范受体结合替代的简单逐步适应性路径。
科研与公卫意义
通过将病毒序列变异与自然进化群体中预测的蛋白质特性联系起来,该框架提供了一种可扩展的策略,用于优先考虑具有跨 宿主 相关特征的 H5N1 变体,以实现目标 监测 和实验随访。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取基于检索到的全文证据。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Standing HA phenotypic breadth shapes H5N1 cross-host potential

Authors: Justin Bahl, M H M Mubassir, Sachin Subedi, Tanin Rajamand, Mohamed Bakheet, Ludy Carmola, Sihua Peng, Rajan Kandel, Guppy Stott, Robert J. Woods
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Linking genetic variation to functional phenotype remains a major barrier to assessing the cross-host potential of emerging viruses. Here, we reconstruct the evolution of predicted hemagglutinin (HA) phenotypic traits across ~13,000 highly pathogenic avian influenza A H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses circulating in North America. The wide geographic spread of avian influenza within North America triggered a wave of broad HA phenotypic diversity that was later refined by the selective sweeps in avian hosts. Following establishment in dairy cattle, however, viral populations exhibited renewed phenotypic diversification in HA, including increased permissiveness for α2,6-linked sialoside engagement despite high conservation of the receptor-binding domain. These patterns indicate that cattle-associated HA phenotypes can draw from standing predicted HA phenotypic breadth within circulating viral populations while continuing to diversify during cattle-associated circulation, rather than following only a simple stepwise adaptive path through canonical receptor-binding substitutions. By linking viral sequence variation to predicted protein properties across naturally evolving populations, this framework provides a scalable strategy for prioritizing H5N1 variants with cross-host-relevant features for targeted surveillance and experimental follow-up.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Abstract Linking genetic variation to functional phenotype remains a major barrier to assessing the cross-host potential of emerging viruses.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
Following establishment in dairy cattle, however, viral populations exhibited renewed phenotypic diversification in HA, including increased permissiveness for α2,6-linked sialoside engagement despite high conservation of the receptor-binding domain.
Interpretation and novelty
These patterns indicate that cattle-associated HA phenotypes can draw from standing predicted HA phenotypic breadth within circulating viral populations while continuing to diversify during cattle-associated circulation, rather than following only a simple stepwise adaptive path through canonical receptor-binding substitutions.
Scientific and public-health significance
By linking viral sequence variation to predicted protein properties across naturally evolving populations, this framework provides a scalable strategy for prioritizing H5N1 variants with cross-host-relevant features for targeted surveillance and experimental follow-up.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on retrieved fulltext evidence. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

中国多省监测期间鸡群中新型H3N8和H3N3 禽流感病毒的出现及其潜在的公共卫生风险。

The emergence of novel H3N8 and H3N3 avian influenza viruses in chickens during multi-province surveillance in China and their potential public health risk.
作者： Xue Wang, Hao Shi, Peidong Li, Hao Zhu, Naijia Zhang, Qingqing Song, Shouchuan Liu, Qingquan Lu, Abdul Khalique, Muhammad Kashif Saleemi
摘要中文翻译H3 亚型 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 由于其高 宿主 适应性和持续的 进化 对全球公共卫生构成重大威胁。最近出现的与跨物种 传播 相关的新型 H3N8 和 H3N3 AIV 强调了加强流行病学 传播 的迫切需要。在本研究中,我们基于 2022 年 11 月至 2023 年 12 月在中国 21 个省份收集的总共 737 份家禽样本,进行了 H3 AIV 的 监测 和表征。其中,69 份 (9.4%) 通过 RT-qPCR 检测出 H3 AIV 阳性,并获得了 1 株 H3N8 分离株和 10 株 H3N3 分离株用于全 基因组 鉴定。我们进行了全 基因组 测序、系统发育分析、重配推断和关键氨基酸取代的评估,同时使用血凝抑制 (HI) 测定和体内小鼠攻击实验进行抗原表征。H3N8分离株被鉴定为三重重组病毒,具有欧亚禽H3基因、北美禽N8基因和H9N2衍生的内部基因。H3N3 分离株代表重配病毒,这些病毒获得了来自新型 H3N8 AIV 谱系的 HA 基因、来自 H10N3 AIV 的 NA 基因以及来自 H9N2 AIV 的内部基因。所有分离株均表现出低致病性 禽流感 病毒的 HA 裂解位点特征。此外,还鉴定了先前与增强哺乳动物适应性相关的几个氨基酸取代,包括PB2中的L89V和I292V以及PB1中的H436Y。在 BALB/c 小鼠攻击实验中,代表性 H3N8 病毒在没有事先适应的情况下就建立了感染,并主要在上呼吸道复制,在呼吸道组织中可检测到病毒 RNA,肺外传播有限。抗原分析显示,通过 HI 测量,新型 H3 AIV 与 H5、H7 或 H9 AIV 之间没有交叉反应性。基于分子和系统发育特征、抗原评估和初步哺乳动物感染数据,我们的研究结果提供了表明潜在公共卫生风险的证据。我们建议在家禽中加强 H3 AIV 的 监测 并加速 疫苗 的开发,以遏制病毒传播并改善公共卫生准备。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
H3亚型禽流感病毒(AIV)由于其高宿主适应性和持续的进化而对全球公共卫生构成重大威胁,而最近出现的与跨物种传播相关的新型H3N8和H3N3 AIV强调了加强流行病学监测的迫切需要。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,监测 和 H3 AIV 的表征是基于 2022 年 11 月至 2023 年 12 月在中国 21 个省份收集的总共 737 个家禽样本进行的。
核心方法
我们进行了全 基因组 测序、系统发育分析、重配推断和关键氨基酸取代的评估,同时使用血凝抑制 (HI) 测定和体内小鼠攻击实验进行抗原表征。
主要结果
在 737 个家禽样本中,69 个(9.4%)通过 RT-qPCR 检测出 H3 AIV 呈阳性,并获得了 1 个 H3N8 分离株和 10 个 H3N3 分离株用于全 基因组 表征;所有分离株均表现出低致病性 禽流感 病毒的 HA 裂解位点特征,并且抗原分析显示,通过 HI 测量,新型 H3 AIV 与 H5、H7 或 H9 AIV 之间没有交叉反应性。
解释与创新
基于分子和系统发育特征、抗原评估和初步哺乳动物感染数据,我们的研究结果提供了证据表明存在潜在的公共卫生风险,因为 H3N8 分离株被鉴定为三重重组病毒,具有欧亚禽 H3 基因、北美禽 N8 基因和 H9N2 衍生的内部基因。
科研与公卫意义
考虑到这些新型病毒带来的潜在公共卫生风险,我们建议在家禽中加强 H3 AIV 的 监测 并加速 疫苗 的开发,以遏制病毒传播并改善公共卫生准备。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究提供了全面的分子和实验数据,但仅限于特定的地理区域和时间范围,需要进一步的研究来评估更广泛的影响。

The emergence of novel H3N8 and H3N3 avian influenza viruses in chickens during multi-province surveillance in China and their potential public health risk.

Authors: Xue Wang, Hao Shi, Peidong Li, Hao Zhu, Naijia Zhang, Qingqing Song, Shouchuan Liu, Qingquan Lu, Abdul Khalique, Muhammad Kashif Saleemi
Original abstract (English)
The H3 subtype avian influenza virus (AIV) poses a substantial global public health threat due to its high host adaptability and ongoing evolution. The recent emergence of novel H3N8 and H3N3 AIVs associated with cross-species transmission underscores the urgent need for enhanced epidemiological surveillance. In this study, we conducted surveillance and characterization of H3 AIVs based on a total of 737 poultry samples collected across 21 Chinese provinces from November 2022 to December 2023. Of these, 69 (9.4%) tested positive for H3 AIV by RT-qPCR, and one H3N8 isolate and ten H3N3 isolates were obtained for whole-genome characterization. We performed whole-genome sequencing, phylogenetic analysis, reassortment inference, and evaluation of key amino acid substitutions, alongside antigenic characterization using hemagglutination inhibition (HI) assays and an in vivo mouse challenge experiment. The H3N8 isolate was identified as a triple-reassortant virus possessing the Eurasian avian H3 gene, the North American avian N8 gene, and H9N2-derived internal genes. The H3N3 isolates represented reassortant viruses that had acquired the HA gene from the novel H3N8 AIV lineage, the NA gene from H10N3 AIV, and internal genes from H9N2 AIV. All isolates exhibited HA cleavage sites characteristic of low pathogenic avian influenza viruses. Additionally, several amino acid substitutions previously associated with enhanced mammalian adaptation were identified, including L89V and I292V in PB2 and H436Y in PB1. In a BALB/c mouse challenge experiment, the representative H3N8 virus established infection without prior adaptation and replicated predominantly in the upper respiratory tract, with detectable viral RNA in respiratory tissues and limited extrapulmonary dissemination. Antigenic analysis revealed no cross-reactivity between the novel H3 AIVs and H5, H7, or H9 AIVs as measured by HI. Based on molecular and phylogenetic characterization, antigenic assessment, and preliminary mammalian infection data, our findings provide evidence suggesting a potential public health risk. We recommend intensified surveillance of H3 AIVs in poultry and accelerated vaccine development to curb viral spread and improve public health preparedness.

Question and background
The H3 subtype avian influenza virus (AIV) poses a substantial global public health threat due to its high host adaptability and ongoing evolution, and the recent emergence of novel H3N8 and H3N3 AIVs associated with cross-species transmission underscores the urgent need for enhanced epidemiological surveillance.
Design and population
In this study, surveillance and characterization of H3 AIVs were conducted based on a total of 737 poultry samples collected across 21 Chinese provinces from November 2022 to December 2023.
Core methods
We performed whole-genome sequencing, phylogenetic analysis, reassortment inference, and evaluation of key amino acid substitutions, alongside antigenic characterization using hemagglutination inhibition (HI) assays and an in vivo mouse challenge experiment.
Main results
Of the 737 poultry samples, 69 (9.4%) tested positive for H3 AIV by RT-qPCR, and one H3N8 isolate and ten H3N3 isolates were obtained for whole-genome characterization; all isolates exhibited HA cleavage sites characteristic of low pathogenic avian influenza viruses, and antigenic analysis revealed no cross-reactivity between the novel H3 AIVs and H5, H7, or H9 AIVs as measured by HI.
Interpretation and novelty

We recommend intensified surveillance of H3 AIVs in poultry and accelerated vaccine development to curb viral spread and improve public health preparedness, given the potential public health risk posed by these novel viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study provides comprehensive molecular and experimental data but is limited to a specific geographic region and time frame, with further studies needed to assess broader implications.

去糖基化 H7N9 VLP 免疫可增强小鼠针对 禽流感 H7N9 变种的跨品系保护性抗体反应

Immunization with deglycosylated H7N9 VLP enhances cross-strain protective antibody responses against avian influenza H7N9 variants in mice
作者： Asmaul Husna, Yu-Chieh Cheng, Chia-Chun Lai, Tsai-Chuan Weng, Min-Han Lin, Min-Shi Lee, Shih-Che Sue, Wang-Chou Sung, Yu‐Chieh Cheng, Shih‐Che Sue
摘要中文翻译简介 快速进化的 禽流感 H7N9 病毒仍然对全球公共卫生构成威胁。迫切需要开发广谱 疫苗,能够引发针对新出现的病毒变种的功能性跨毒株保护性免疫。方法 N-连接糖基化可以保护关键残基免受免疫识别。在这里,基于 A/Taiwan/1/2013 H7N9 病毒的抗原序列构建了原型病毒样颗粒 (VLP)。利用糖苷酶修剪聚糖分子,以生成单糖基化 VLP (VLPmg) 和非糖基化 VLP (VLPng) 衍生物。对这些 VLP 的结构完整性、抗原性和免疫原性进行了系统评估。结果 VLPmg 和 VLPng 均保持与完全糖基化原型 VLP (VLPfg) 一样的血凝活性和抗原性。用去糖基化的 VLP 进行免疫可以在小鼠体内诱导出强大的病毒特异性抗体和平衡的 Th1/Th2 免疫特征,而滴度与 VLPfg 相当。其中,VLPng针对不同的H7N9病毒株诱导了更高的血凝抑制和中和抗体滴度,表明跨病毒株反应性抗体反应得到改善。结论 这项研究表明,流感 H7N9 VLP 的酶促去糖基化可改善小鼠体内广泛保护性抗体反应的诱导。非糖基化的 H7N9 VLP 代表了潜在的 疫苗 候选药物,可对抗抗原不同的 H7N9 病毒变种的威胁。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决快速进化的 禽流感 H7N9 病毒对全球公共卫生构成的威胁,并探索开发能够针对新出现的变种引发广谱保护性免疫力的 疫苗。
设计与对象
基于A/Taiwan/1/2013 H7N9病毒的抗原序列构建了原型病毒样颗粒(VLP),并在小鼠中系统评价了这些VLP的结构完整性、抗原性和免疫原性。
核心方法
N-连接糖基化被确定为一种保护关键残基免受免疫识别的机制,并使用糖苷酶来修剪聚糖分子以生成单糖基化 VLP (VLPmg) 和非糖基化 VLP (VLPng) 衍生物。
主要结果
VLPmg 和 VLPng 均保持与完全糖基化原型 VLP (VLPfg) 相似的血凝活性和抗原性。使用去糖基化的 VLP 进行免疫可以在小鼠体内诱导出强大的病毒特异性抗体和平衡的 Th1/Th2 免疫特征,滴度与 VLPfg 相当。
解释与创新
该研究表明,流感 H7N9 VLP 的酶促去糖基化可改善小鼠体内广泛保护性抗体反应的诱导,凸显了 疫苗 开发的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,非糖基化的 H7N9 VLP 可以作为 疫苗 的潜在候选者,以对抗抗原不同的 H7N9 病毒变种的威胁,为改进 监测 和 疫苗 设计提供启示。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠体内实验,结果可能无法直接转化为人类免疫力。由于缺乏临床数据且依赖动物模型,证据强度中等。

Immunization with deglycosylated H7N9 VLP enhances cross-strain protective antibody responses against avian influenza H7N9 variants in mice

Authors: Asmaul Husna, Yu-Chieh Cheng, Chia-Chun Lai, Tsai-Chuan Weng, Min-Han Lin, Min-Shi Lee, Shih-Che Sue, Wang-Chou Sung, Yu‐Chieh Cheng, Shih‐Che Sue
Original abstract (English)
Introduction The rapidly evolving avian influenza H7N9 virus remains a threaten to public health globally. There is an urgent need to develop broad-spectrum vaccines capable of eliciting functional cross-strain protective immunity against emerging viral variants. Methods N-linked glycosylation can shield critical residues from immune recognition. Here, a prototype virus-like particle (VLP) was constructed based on the antigenic sequences derived from A/Taiwan/1/2013 H7N9 virus. Glycosidase enzymes were utilized to trim the glycan molecules for generating monoglycosylated VLP (VLPmg) and nonglycosylated VLP (VLPng) derivates. The structural integrity, antigenicity, and immunogenicity of these VLPs were systematically evaluated. Results Both VLPmg and VLPng maintained hemagglutination activity and antigenicity like the fully glycosylated prototype VLP (VLPfg). Immunization with deglycosylated VLPs induced robust virus-specific antibodies and a balanced Th1/Th2 immune profile in mice, whereas the titers were comparable to that of VLPfg. Among them, VLPng induced higher hemagglutination inhibition and neutralizing antibody titers against different H7N9 virus strains, indicating improved cross-strain reactive antibody responses. Conclusion This study demonstrated the enzymatic deglycosylation of influenza H7N9 VLP improves the induction of broadly protective antibody responses in mice. The nonglycosylated H7N9 VLP represents a potential vaccine candidate to against the threat of antigenically divergent H7N9 virus variants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the threat posed by the rapidly evolving avian influenza H7N9 virus to global public health and to explore the development of vaccines capable of eliciting broad-spectrum protective immunity against emerging variants.
Design and population
A prototype virus-like particle (VLP) was constructed based on the antigenic sequences from A/Taiwan/1/2013 H7N9 virus, and the structural integrity, antigenicity, and immunogenicity of these VLPs were systematically evaluated in mice.
Core methods
N-linked glycosylation was identified as a mechanism that shields critical residues from immune recognition, and glycosidase enzymes were used to trim glycan molecules to generate monoglycosylated VLP (VLPmg) and nonglycosylated VLP (VLPng) derivates.
Main results
Both VLPmg and VLPng maintained hemagglutination activity and antigenicity similar to the fully glycosylated prototype VLP (VLPfg). Immunization with deglycosylated VLPs induced robust virus-specific antibodies and a balanced Th1/Th2 immune profile in mice, with titers comparable to that of VLPfg.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated that enzymatic deglycosylation of influenza H7N9 VLP improves the induction of broadly protective antibody responses in mice, highlighting a novel approach to vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that nonglycosylated H7N9 VLPs could serve as a potential vaccine candidate to combat the threat of antigenically divergent H7N9 virus variants, offering implications for improved surveillance and vaccine design.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vivo experiments in mice, and the results may not directly translate to human immunity. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of clinical data and the reliance on animal models.

哥斯达黎加后院鸡中 H5N1 HPAIV 分支 2.3.4.4b 禽流感 病毒的检测

Detection of H5N1 HPAIV Clade 2.3.4.4b Avian Influenza Virus in Backyard Chickens in Costa Rica
作者： Bernal León, Hebleen Brenes, Nidia S. Trovao, Olga Aguilar, Fabián Carvajal, Idania Chacón, Guisella Chaves, Mónica Guzmán, Claudio Soto-Garita, Estela Cordero
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒是一种分段的负义RNA病毒。自 2020 年代初以来,H5 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒已在欧洲、非洲和亚洲广泛传播,影响 野生鸟类 和家禽。哥斯达黎加于 2023 年 1 月 19 日报告了首例 H5 分支 2.3.4.4b 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 病例。这项研究描述了后院鸡和鸭中的 暴发。1 月 24 日收集的初始血清样本显示,三只鸡的 AIV 呈阴性,而一只鸭通过 ELISA 和琼脂凝胶免疫扩散 (AGID) 检测呈阳性。在 1 月 27 日的第二次访问中,收集的四份鸡血清中的三份经 ELISA 和 AGID 检测呈阳性。qRT-PCR 检测组织样本甲型流感呈阳性。新一代测序恢复了八个病毒基因组片段中的五个,血凝素裂解位点序列 (REKRRKR↓G) 证实了 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) H5 株。样本已提交给国家兽医服务实验室进行确认。血清学检测显示对北美低致病性 H5 抗原有反应性,qRT-PCR 扩增了甲型和 N1 型流感基因。所有八个病毒 基因组 片段的病毒分离和下一代测序 (NGS) 均在圣裘德儿童研究医院 (SJCRH) 的世卫组织合作中心成功进行。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
继 2023 年 1 月 19 日在哥斯达黎加报告首例病例后,需要开展这项研究来检测和表征哥斯达黎加后院鸡中的 H5N1 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 分支 2.3.4.4b。
设计与对象
该研究描述了后院鸡和鸭中的 暴发,样本于 2023 年 1 月 24 日至 27 日采集,并提交给国家兽医服务实验室进行确认。
核心方法
通过 ELISA 和琼脂凝胶免疫扩散 (AGID) 测试初始血清样本,通过 qRT-PCR 测试组织样本,通过新一代测序测试病毒基因组片段;病毒分离和全 基因组 测序是在圣裘德儿童研究医院的世卫组织合作中心进行的。
主要结果
1 月 24 日,三只鸡的 AIV 检测呈阴性,而一只鸭的检测呈阳性;1月27日,4份鸡血清中的3份通过ELISA和AGID检测呈阳性;组织样本经 qRT-PCR 检测呈甲型流感阳性,测序证实为高致病性 H5 毒株,其血凝素裂解位点序列为 REKRRKR↓G。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 H5N1 HPAIV 分支 2.3.4.4b 在哥斯达黎加后院家禽中循环的证据,这是该国首次检测到该菌株,并强调其已传播到新地区。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 监测 在中美洲的 禽流感 工作提供了信息,强调需要监测后院家禽并及早发现,以防止潜在的人畜共患 传播 和 暴发。
局限与证据强度
尽管后来在参考实验室进行了全 基因组 测序,但由于样本量有限且最初无法对所有八个病毒基因组片段进行测序,证据强度中等。

Detection of H5N1 HPAIV Clade 2.3.4.4b Avian Influenza Virus in Backyard Chickens in Costa Rica

Authors: Bernal León, Hebleen Brenes, Nidia S. Trovao, Olga Aguilar, Fabián Carvajal, Idania Chacón, Guisella Chaves, Mónica Guzmán, Claudio Soto-Garita, Estela Cordero
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus is a segmented, negative-sense RNA virus. Since the early 2020s, H5 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have spread widely across Europe, Africa, and Asia, affecting wild birds and poultry. Costa Rica reported its first H5 clade 2.3.4.4b avian influenza virus (AIV) case on 19 January 2023. This study describes an outbreak in backyard chickens and ducks. Initial serum samples collected on 24 January showed three chickens negative for AIV, while one duck tested positive by ELISA and agar gel immunodiffusion (AGID). During a second visit on 27 January, three of four chicken sera collected tested positive by ELISA and AGID. Tissue samples were positive for influenza A by qRT-PCR. Next-generation sequencing recovered five of the eight viral genomic segments, and the hemagglutinin cleavage site sequence (REKRRKR↓G) confirmed a highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) H5 strain. The samples were submitted to the National Veterinary Services Laboratories for confirmation. Serological testing showed reactivity to North American low pathogenic H5 antigens, and qRT-PCR amplified influenza A and N1 genes. Virus isolation and next-generation sequencing (NGS) of all eight viral genome segments were successfully performed at the WHO Collaborating Centre at St. Jude Children’s Research Hospital (SJCRH).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study was needed to detect and characterize H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) clade 2.3.4.4b in backyard chickens in Costa Rica, following its first reported case in the country on 19 January 2023.
Design and population
The study describes an outbreak in backyard chickens and ducks, with samples collected on 24 and 27 January 2023 and submitted to the National Veterinary Services Laboratories for confirmation.
Core methods
Initial serum samples were tested by ELISA and agar gel immunodiffusion (AGID), tissue samples by qRT-PCR, and viral genomic segments by next-generation sequencing; virus isolation and full-genome sequencing were performed at the WHO Collaborating Centre at St. Jude Children's Research Hospital.
Main results
Three chickens tested negative for AIV on 24 January, while one duck tested positive; on 27 January, three of four chicken sera tested positive by ELISA and AGID; tissue samples were positive for influenza A by qRT-PCR, and sequencing confirmed a highly pathogenic H5 strain with the hemagglutinin cleavage site sequence REKRRKR↓G.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence of H5N1 HPAIV clade 2.3.4.4b circulation in backyard poultry in Costa Rica, representing the first detection of this strain in the country and highlighting its spread to new regions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform surveillance efforts for avian influenza in Central America, emphasizing the need for monitoring backyard poultry and early detection to prevent potential zoonotic transmission and outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the limited sample size and the inability to sequence all eight viral genomic segments initially, though full-genome sequencing was later performed at a reference laboratory.

罗马尼亚H5N1 禽流感株PB1和PB2基因的遗传决定因素分析

Genetic Determinants Analysis of PB1 and PB2 Genes in H5N1 Avian Influenza Strains from Romania
作者： Maria Rodica Gurău, Florica Bărbuceanu, Alexandru Gligor, Cosmin Șonea, Georgeta Ștefan, Doina Daneș, Elena Negru, Iosif Nicodim Fiț, Kalmár Zsuzsa, Doru Valentin Hristescu
摘要中文翻译禽流感 是一种影响国内和 野生鸟类 的高度传染性病毒性疾病,由正粘病毒科 A 型流感病毒引起。连续遗传 监测 对于监测病毒 进化 和识别与致病性和 宿主 适应相关的分子决定因素至关重要。在这项研究中,对 2017 年至 2024 年在罗马尼亚检测到的 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 病毒的 PB1 和 PB2 聚合酶基因进行了基因表征。对从国内和罗马尼亚几个县的 野生鸟类 收集的总共 18 个器官样本进行实时 RT-PCR 分析,然后进行常规 RT-PCR、Sanger 测序、突变分析和系统发育重建。获得了 6 个 PB1 和 8 个 PB2 基因片段的高质量序列数据。序列分析揭示了 PB1 基因内的高度保守性,特别是在涉及病毒复制的区域之间,这表明存在强大的进化限制。相比之下,PB2 基因表现出多个氨基酸取代,包括选定分离株中 464 和 678 位的变化。值得注意的是,所有分析的菌株都缺乏哺乳动物适应标记 E627K。系统发育分析将罗马尼亚分离株聚集在欧亚 H5N1 谱系中,并证明与东欧和俄罗斯流行的当代毒株有密切的遗传关系。这些发现强调了 PB1 的遗传稳定性、PB2 的持续多样化,以及连续分子 监测 对于早期检测病毒变异的重要性,这对 宿主 适应和人畜共患风险具有潜在影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 是一种影响国内和 野生鸟类 的高度传染性病毒性疾病,由正粘病毒科 A 型流感病毒引起。连续遗传 监测 对于监测病毒 进化 和识别与致病性和 宿主 适应相关的分子决定因素至关重要。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,对 2017 年至 2024 年在罗马尼亚检测到的 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 病毒的 PB1 和 PB2 聚合酶基因进行了基因表征。对从国内和罗马尼亚几个县的 野生鸟类 采集的总共 18 个器官样本进行了分析。
核心方法
获得了 6 个 PB1 和 8 个 PB2 基因片段的高质量序列数据。通过实时 RT-PCR 分析样品,然后进行常规 RT-PCR、桑格测序、突变分析和系统发育重建。
主要结果
序列分析揭示了 PB1 基因内的高度保守性,特别是在涉及病毒复制的区域之间,这表明存在强大的进化限制。相比之下,PB2 基因表现出多个氨基酸取代,包括选定分离株中 464 和 678 位的变化。值得注意的是,所有分析的菌株都缺乏哺乳动物适应标记 E627K。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了 PB1 的遗传稳定性、PB2 的持续多样化,以及连续分子 监测 对于早期检测病毒变异的重要性,这对 宿主 适应和人畜共患病风险具有潜在影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果通过强调需要监测 PB2 突变 的 宿主 适应标记和人畜共患潜力,为 监测 策略提供信息。与东欧和俄罗斯菌株的遗传关系凸显了跨境 传播 风险。
局限与证据强度
由于样本量有限(18 个样本)并且重点关注两个聚合酶基因,该研究的证据强度中等。功能测定的缺乏限制了对观察到的 突变 生物学意义的解释。

Genetic Determinants Analysis of PB1 and PB2 Genes in H5N1 Avian Influenza Strains from Romania

Authors: Maria Rodica Gurău, Florica Bărbuceanu, Alexandru Gligor, Cosmin Șonea, Georgeta Ștefan, Doina Daneș, Elena Negru, Iosif Nicodim Fiț, Kalmár Zsuzsa, Doru Valentin Hristescu
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza is a highly contagious viral disease affecting domestic and wild birds, caused by influenza A viruses of the family Orthomyxoviridae. Continuous genetic surveillance is essential for monitoring viral evolution and identifying molecular determinants associated with pathogenicity and host adaptation. In this study, the PB1 and PB2 polymerase genes of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 viruses detected in Romania between 2017 and 2024 were genetically characterized. A total of 18 organ samples collected from domestic and wild birds in several Romanian counties were analyzed by real-time RT-PCR, followed by conventional RT-PCR, Sanger sequencing, mutational analysis, and phylogenetic reconstruction. High-quality sequence data were obtained for six PB1 and eight PB2 gene fragments. Sequence analysis revealed a high degree of conservation within the PB1 gene, particularly across regions involved in viral replication, indicating strong evolutionary constraints. In contrast, the PB2 gene exhibited several amino acid substitutions, including changes at positions 464 and 678 in selected isolates. Notably, all analyzed strains lacked the mammalian adaptation marker E627K. Phylogenetic analysis clustered the Romanian isolates within Eurasian H5N1 lineages and demonstrated close genetic relationships with contemporary strains circulating in Eastern Europe and Russia. These findings highlight the genetic stability of PB1, the ongoing diversification of PB2, and the importance of continuous molecular surveillance for the early detection of viral variants with potential implications for host adaptation and zoonotic risk.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza is a highly contagious viral disease affecting domestic and wild birds, caused by influenza A viruses of the family Orthomyxoviridae. Continuous genetic surveillance is essential for monitoring viral evolution and identifying molecular determinants associated with pathogenicity and host adaptation.
Design and population
In this study, the PB1 and PB2 polymerase genes of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 viruses detected in Romania between 2017 and 2024 were genetically characterized. A total of 18 organ samples collected from domestic and wild birds in several Romanian counties were analyzed.
Core methods
High-quality sequence data were obtained for six PB1 and eight PB2 gene fragments. Samples were analyzed by real-time RT-PCR, followed by conventional RT-PCR, Sanger sequencing, mutational analysis, and phylogenetic reconstruction.
Main results
Sequence analysis revealed a high degree of conservation within the PB1 gene, particularly across regions involved in viral replication, indicating strong evolutionary constraints. In contrast, the PB2 gene exhibited several amino acid substitutions, including changes at positions 464 and 678 in selected isolates. Notably, all analyzed strains lacked the mammalian adaptation marker E627K.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the genetic stability of PB1, the ongoing diversification of PB2, and the importance of continuous molecular surveillance for the early detection of viral variants with potential implications for host adaptation and zoonotic risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results inform surveillance strategies by emphasizing the need to monitor PB2 mutations for host adaptation markers and zoonotic potential. The genetic relationships with Eastern European and Russian strains highlight cross-border transmission risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate due to the limited sample size (18 samples) and focus on two polymerase genes. The absence of functional assays limits the interpretation of the biological significance of observed mutations.

2026 年,斯瓦尔巴特群岛,北极熊和大西洋海象体内的 高致病性禽流感 H5N5,在北极熊体内出现广泛的血清转化

Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza H5N5 in a Polar Bear and Atlantic Walrus, Svalbard, 2026, with Widespread Seroconversion in Polar Bears
作者： Knut Madslien, Johanna Hol Fosse, Jon Aars, Cathrine Arnason Bøe, M Oerbaek Andersen, Kayla J. Buhler, Ingvild Fjeldheim, Britt Gjerset, Torild Jørgensen, Ida Kristin Myhrvold
摘要中文翻译摘要 在一只一岁的北极熊 (Ursus maritimus) 和一只邻近的成年大西洋海象 (Odobenus rosmarus rosmarus) 中检测到 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 亚型 H5N5,这两只动物均在斯瓦尔巴群岛的 Raudfjorden 死亡。这是欧洲北极熊中首例确诊的高致病性禽流感病例,也是大西洋海象中的第二例确诊病例。病毒 基因组 几乎相同,并含有 PB2-E627V,这是一种与哺乳动物适应相关的标记。此前曾观察到包括死者在内的几只北极熊以海象尸体为食。2023 年,75% 的北极熊 (n=36) 检测到了 H5 抗体,2024-2025 年,这一比例为 97% (n=65),这表明自 2022 年在斯瓦尔巴群岛的鸟类中首次检测到 HPAIV 后,HPAIV 在北极熊种群中广泛传播,而 2014-2022 年的样本中未检测到 H5 抗体 (n=243)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 高致病性禽流感 H5N5 在斯瓦尔巴群岛的北极野生动物(特别是北极熊和大西洋海象)中的出现和传播,继 2022 年在鸟类中首次检测到后。
设计与对象
该研究检查了斯瓦尔巴群岛劳德峡湾死去的北极熊和大西洋海象,并对 2014 年至 2025 年的北极熊种群进行了血清学检测。
核心方法
对病毒 基因组 进行测序以鉴定 基因组,并使用未指定的方法进行血清学测试以检测北极熊样本中的 H5 抗体。
主要结果
在一岁北极熊和成年大西洋海象身上检测到 HPAIV H5N5;病毒 基因组 几乎相同,并含有 PB2-E627V,一种哺乳动物适应标记;2023 年接受测试的北极熊中有 75% 拥有 H5 抗体,2024-2025 年则有 97% 拥有 H5 抗体,而 2014-2022 年则为零。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为继鸟类 跨物种溢出 之后北极熊体内广泛 HPAIV 循环的证据,其中 PB2-E627V 突变 表明哺乳动物的适应;这是首例欧洲北极熊确诊的高致病性禽流感病例,也是第二例大西洋海象确诊的高致病性禽流感病例。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了 HPAIV 传播 对海洋哺乳动物和北极野生动物的风险,强调极地地区需要加强 监测 来监测病毒 进化 和潜在的人畜共患威胁。
局限与证据强度
由于死亡动物的样本量较小且依赖于未经病毒分离的血清学数据,证据强度中等;局限性包括无法确认 传播 路线以及北极熊种群中潜在的抽样偏差。

Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza H5N5 in a Polar Bear and Atlantic Walrus, Svalbard, 2026, with Widespread Seroconversion in Polar Bears

Authors: Knut Madslien, Johanna Hol Fosse, Jon Aars, Cathrine Arnason Bøe, M Oerbaek Andersen, Kayla J. Buhler, Ingvild Fjeldheim, Britt Gjerset, Torild Jørgensen, Ida Kristin Myhrvold
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) subtype H5N5 was detected in a one-year-old polar bear ( Ursus maritimus ) and an adjacent adult Atlantic walrus ( Odobenus rosmarus rosmarus ), both found deceased in Raudfjorden, Svalbard. This represents the first confirmed case of HPAI in a European polar bear and the second in an Atlantic walrus. Viral genomes were nearly identical and harbored PB2-E627V, a marker associated with mammalian adaptation. Several polar bears, including the deceased individual, had previously been observed feeding on the walrus carcass. Antibodies against H5 were detected in 75% of polar bears in 2023 (n=36) and 97% in 2024-2025 (n=65), suggesting extensive circulation of HPAIV in the population following the first detections in birds in Svalbard in 2022, whereas no antibodies were detected in samples from 2014-2022 (n=243).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the emergence and spread of highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N5 in Arctic wildlife, specifically polar bears and Atlantic walruses, in Svalbard, following initial detections in birds in 2022.
Design and population
The study examined deceased polar bears and Atlantic walruses in Raudfjorden, Svalbard, and conducted serological testing on polar bear populations from 2014 to 2025.
Core methods
Viral genomes were sequenced to identify mutations, and serological testing was performed to detect H5 antibodies in polar bear samples using unspecified methods.
Main results
HPAIV H5N5 was detected in a one-year-old polar bear and an adult Atlantic walrus; viral genomes were nearly identical and contained PB2-E627V, a mammalian adaptation marker; 75% of polar bears tested in 2023 and 97% in 2024-2025 had H5 antibodies, compared to zero in 2014-2022.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence of extensive HPAIV circulation in polar bears following spillover from birds, with the PB2-E627V mutation suggesting mammalian adaptation; this represents the first confirmed HPAI case in a European polar bear and second in an Atlantic walrus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the risk of HPAIV transmission to marine mammals and Arctic wildlife, underscoring the need for enhanced surveillance in polar regions to monitor viral evolution and potential zoonotic threats.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size of deceased animals and reliance on serological data without viral isolation; limitations include the inability to confirm transmission routes and potential sampling bias in polar bear populations.

牛源性 H5N1 流感病毒通过乳腺有效感染哺乳猪

Bovine-derived H5N1 influenza virus efficiently infects lactating swine via the mammary gland
作者： Min Liu, Natalie N. Chillson, Emma A. Martin, Hannah J. Cochran, Janice Y. Park, Brady O’Boyle, Devra Huey, Kara N. Corps, Andrew S. Bowman, Cody J. Warren
摘要中文翻译摘要 自 2024 年以来,高致病性甲型流感 (H5N1) 病毒在美国奶牛中广泛传播,它们在乳腺中高效复制,并在牛奶中以高滴度传播。为了直接评估商业猪群对牛源性 H5N1 病毒的敏感性,通过乳房内途径对具有代表美国商业猪生产系统的既往流感病毒 疫苗接种 历史的哺乳母猪进行接种,并与 1 周龄仔猪共同饲养,以评估疾病结果、病毒复制和垂直 传播 的潜力。哺乳母猪的乳房内接种导致乳汁中病毒 RNA 持续脱落,而仔猪则表现出零星的口腔病毒 RNA 阳性,这反映了乳汁中的病毒动力学。乳腺组织和病毒抗原染色的病变,以及中和抗体反应的发展以及牛奶颜色和稠度的变化,进一步证实了母猪的感染。尽管有这些分子发现,但没有一只动物出现明显的临床疾病,并且在研究期间没有注意到呼吸系统受累。总的来说,我们证明,尽管哺乳母猪有 疫苗接种 历史,但乳房内暴露会导致甲型 (H5N1) 流感病毒感染,这表明病毒 跨物种溢出 对商业猪群存在潜在威胁。临床上不明显的感染性质存在亚临床传播的风险,并强调了将病毒 监测 扩展到猪的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于自 2024 年以来高致病性甲型流感 (H5N1) 病毒在美国奶牛中广泛传播,需要开展这项研究来评估商业猪群对牛源性 H5N1 流感病毒的易感性。
设计与对象
进行了一项研究,其中代表美国商业养猪生产系统的既往有流感病毒 疫苗接种 历史的哺乳母猪通过乳房内途径接种,并与 1 周大的仔猪共同饲养。
核心方法
哺乳母猪通过乳房内途径接种,通过监测牛奶中病毒 RNA 脱落、乳腺组织病变、病毒抗原染色、中和抗体反应以及牛奶颜色和稠度变化来评估疾病结果、病毒复制和垂直 传播 的潜力。
主要结果
乳房内接种导致乳汁中病毒 RNA 持续脱落,而仔猪则表现出零星的口腔病毒 RNA 阳性,这反映了乳汁中的病毒动力学;乳腺组织的病变和病毒抗原染色,以及中和抗体反应的发展以及牛奶颜色和稠度的变化,证实了母猪的感染。
解释与创新
作者解释说,尽管哺乳母猪有 疫苗接种 病史,但乳房内暴露会导致甲型 H5N1 流感病毒感染,这表明病毒 跨物种溢出 对商业猪群存在潜在威胁,而且临床上不明显的感染性质存在亚临床传播的风险。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了将 监测 病毒扩展到猪群以防止潜在的亚临床传播和 H5N1 病毒从牛到猪的 跨物种溢出 的重要性。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究是在有限数量的动物的受控环境中进行的,并且缺乏明显的临床疾病和呼吸系统受累可能限制了对现场条件的推广。

Bovine-derived H5N1 influenza virus efficiently infects lactating swine via the mammary gland

Authors: Min Liu, Natalie N. Chillson, Emma A. Martin, Hannah J. Cochran, Janice Y. Park, Brady O’Boyle, Devra Huey, Kara N. Corps, Andrew S. Bowman, Cody J. Warren
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Since 2024, highly pathogenic influenza A(H5N1) viruses have spread extensively among U.S. dairy cattle, where they replicate efficiently in the mammary gland and are shed at high titers in milk. To directly assess susceptibility of commercial swine populations to bovine-derived H5N1 virus, lactating sows with prior influenza virus vaccination histories representative of U.S. commercial swine production systems were inoculated via the intramammary route and co-housed with their 1-week-old piglets to evaluate disease outcomes, viral replication, and potential for vertical transmission. Intramammary inoculation of lactating sows resulted in sustained viral RNA shedding in milk, while piglets exhibited sporadic oral viral RNA positivity that mirrored viral kinetics in milk. Lesions in mammary tissue and viral antigen staining, as well as development of neutralizing antibody responses and changes in milk color and consistency, further confirmed infection in the sows. Despite these molecular findings, none of the animals developed overt clinical disease, and respiratory involvement was not noted during the study period. Collectively, we demonstrate that intramammary exposure results in productive influenza A(H5N1) virus infection in lactating sows despite their vaccination histories, indicating the potential threat of viral spillover into commercial swine populations. The clinically inapparent nature of infection presents a risk of subclinical spread and underscores the importance of expanding viral surveillance to swine.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to assess the susceptibility of commercial swine populations to bovine-derived H5N1 influenza virus, given the extensive spread of highly pathogenic influenza A(H5N1) viruses among U.S. dairy cattle since 2024.
Design and population
A study was conducted where lactating sows with prior influenza virus vaccination histories representative of U.S. commercial swine production systems were inoculated via the intramammary route and co-housed with their 1-week-old piglets.
Core methods
Lactating sows were inoculated via the intramammary route, and disease outcomes, viral replication, and potential for vertical transmission were evaluated by monitoring viral RNA shedding in milk, lesions in mammary tissue, viral antigen staining, neutralizing antibody responses, and milk color and consistency changes.
Main results
Intramammary inoculation resulted in sustained viral RNA shedding in milk, while piglets exhibited sporadic oral viral RNA positivity that mirrored viral kinetics in milk; lesions in mammary tissue and viral antigen staining, as well as development of neutralizing antibody responses and changes in milk color and consistency, confirmed infection in the sows.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that intramammary exposure results in productive influenza A(H5N1) virus infection in lactating sows despite their vaccination histories, indicating the potential threat of viral spillover into commercial swine populations, and the clinically inapparent nature of infection presents a risk of subclinical spread.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of expanding viral surveillance to swine populations to prevent potential subclinical spread and spillover of H5N1 virus from cattle to swine.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study was conducted in a controlled setting with a limited number of animals, and the absence of overt clinical disease and respiratory involvement may limit generalizability to field conditions.

鉴定针对 H9N2 禽流感 病毒血凝素蛋白的两个新型 B 细胞表位

Identification of two novel B-cell epitopes targeting the hemagglutinin protein of H9N2 avian influenza virus
作者： Dong Chen, Hongyun Wei, Yanan Zhang, Dan Liu, Dongni Kong, Zahra Zeinalzadeh, Ke Li, Peng Zhou, Hui Jin, Hongbo Zhou
摘要中文翻译摘要 H9N2 禽流感 病毒(AIV)仍然对家禽健康构成全球性威胁,并具有人畜共患病的可能性。血凝素 (HA) 蛋白的抗原漂移使 疫苗 的功效和诊断准确性变得复杂,凸显了精确表位表征的必要性。在本研究中,H9N2 AIV的HA蛋白在真核系统中表达,并产生了两种单克隆抗体(mAb):9C12和9F4。ELISA、蛋白质印迹和免疫荧光显示,两种 mAb 均特异性结合 HA,但缺乏血凝抑制活性。表位作图揭示了两个最小的线性表位:残留酯酶结构域内的 123 FSSSRSYQ 130 和受体结合结构域内的 201 NLYTRTDTT 209。丙氨酸扫描揭示了抗体结合所需的关键残基,而结构模型证实了两个表位都暴露在表面。序列分析表明,H9N2 毒株之间具有很强的保守性,其中 9F4 表位显示出近乎完全的不变性,而这两个表位在其他甲型流感病毒亚型中都表现出较低的保守性。这些发现定义了 H9N2 HA 上的两个新颖的非中和表位,它们扩展了抗原图谱,并代表了亚型特异性诊断检测的有希望的目标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
H9N2 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 仍然对家禽健康构成全球性威胁,并具有人畜共患的潜力,血凝素 (HA) 蛋白的抗原漂移使 疫苗 的功效和诊断准确性变得复杂,凸显了精确表位表征的必要性。
设计与对象
在本研究中,H9N2 AIV的HA蛋白在真核系统中表达,并产生了两种单克隆抗体(mAb):9C12和9F4。
核心方法
ELISA、蛋白质印迹和免疫荧光显示,两种 mAb 都特异性结合 HA,但缺乏血凝抑制活性,并且进行表位作图以鉴定最小线性表位。
主要结果
表位作图揭示了两个最小的线性表位:残留酯酶结构域内的 123 FSSSRSYQ 130 和受体结合结构域内的 201 NLYTRTDTT 209,丙氨酸扫描鉴定了抗体结合所需的关键残基。
解释与创新
这些发现定义了 H9N2 HA 上的两个新颖的非中和表位,它们扩展了抗原图谱,并代表了亚型特异性诊断检测的有希望的目标。
科研与公卫意义
鉴定出的表位可以提高 H9N2 AIV 的诊断准确性,并有助于亚型特异性 疫苗 和 监测 工具的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究侧重于表位的体外表征,并未评估其在体内或现场条件下的效用;然而,由于严格的实验验证,证据强度很高。

Identification of two novel B-cell epitopes targeting the hemagglutinin protein of H9N2 avian influenza virus

Authors: Dong Chen, Hongyun Wei, Yanan Zhang, Dan Liu, Dongni Kong, Zahra Zeinalzadeh, Ke Li, Peng Zhou, Hui Jin, Hongbo Zhou
Original abstract (English)
Abstract H9N2 avian influenza virus (AIV) remains a global threat to poultry health and has zoonotic potential. Antigenic drift in the hemagglutinin (HA) protein complicates vaccine efficacy and diagnostic accuracy, highlighting the need for precise epitope characterization. In this study, the HA protein of H9N2 AIV was expressed in a eukaryotic system, and two monoclonal antibodies (mAbs), 9C12 and 9F4, were generated. Both mAbs specifically bound HA, as shown by ELISA, Western blot, and immunofluorescence, but lacked hemagglutination inhibition activity. Epitope mapping revealed two minimal linear epitopes: 123 FSSSRSYQ 130 within the vestigial esterase domain and 201 NLYTRTDTT 209 within the receptor-binding domain. Alanine scanning revealed key residues required for antibody binding, whereas structural modeling confirmed that both epitopes are surface exposed. Sequence analysis demonstrated strong conservation across H9N2 strains, with the 9F4 epitope showing near-complete invariance, whereas both epitopes exhibited low conservation among other influenza A virus subtypes. These findings define two novel, nonneutralizing epitopes on H9N2 HA that expand the antigenic map and represent promising targets for subtype-specific diagnostic assays.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
H9N2 avian influenza virus (AIV) remains a global threat to poultry health and has zoonotic potential, and antigenic drift in the hemagglutinin (HA) protein complicates vaccine efficacy and diagnostic accuracy, highlighting the need for precise epitope characterization.
Design and population
In this study, the HA protein of H9N2 AIV was expressed in a eukaryotic system, and two monoclonal antibodies (mAbs), 9C12 and 9F4, were generated.
Core methods
Both mAbs specifically bound HA, as shown by ELISA, Western blot, and immunofluorescence, but lacked hemagglutination inhibition activity, and epitope mapping was performed to identify minimal linear epitopes.
Main results
Epitope mapping revealed two minimal linear epitopes: 123 FSSSRSYQ 130 within the vestigial esterase domain and 201 NLYTRTDTT 209 within the receptor-binding domain, and alanine scanning identified key residues required for antibody binding.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings define two novel, nonneutralizing epitopes on H9N2 HA that expand the antigenic map and represent promising targets for subtype-specific diagnostic assays.
Scientific and public-health significance
The identified epitopes could improve diagnostic accuracy for H9N2 AIV and contribute to the development of subtype-specific vaccines and surveillance tools.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study focused on in vitro characterization of epitopes and did not evaluate their utility in vivo or in field conditions; however, the evidence strength is high due to rigorous experimental validation.

使用不同波长的深紫外线辐射灭活高致病性禽流感病毒波长の异なる深による高病原性鸟インエンザウイルスの不活化

Academic paper (Japanese original; verified English title unavailable)
作者： Ryosuke MATSUURA, Akatsuki SAITO, Fumihiro NAGATA, Noriko FUKUSHI, Raita HORIGUCHI, Kunio KANEMARU, Yuji ODA, Koichi MATSUO, Yoko AIDA
摘要中文翻译据报道,H5N1 亚型的 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 不仅感染鸟类,还感染人类、牛和其他哺乳动物,造成严重的公共卫生问题,并强调采取有效控制措施的必要性。紫外线 (UV) 照射被广泛认为是灭活病毒的有效方式,而 LED 技术的最新进展已经能够产生各种波长的紫外线,每种波长都具有独特的特性。在这项研究中,我们评估了四种不同紫外线波长(222 nm、230 nm、254 nm 和 265 nm)灭活 HPAIV 的功效。用剂量范围为 0 至 7.3 mJ/cm2 的紫外线照射浓度为 2.0 × 106 噬菌斑形成单位 (PFU) /mL 的 HPAIV。对于 222 nm、230 nm、254 nm 和 265 nm 的紫外线波长,实现病毒滴度降低 99.9% 所需的紫外线剂量分别为 4.2 mJ、4.9 mJ、5.6 mJ 和 4.3 mJ。所有四种紫外线波长均可将病毒滴度降低 99.9% 以上,证明其灭活 HPAIV 的有效性。这项研究表明,所有四个测试波长的紫外线都能在相当程度上有效地灭活 HPAIV,这表明紫外线可在多种环境中灭活 HPAIV。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
据报道,H5N1 亚型的 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 不仅感染鸟类,还感染人类、牛和其他哺乳动物,造成严重的公共卫生问题,并强调采取有效控制措施的必要性。
设计与对象
紫外线 (UV) 照射被广泛认为是灭活病毒的有效方式,而 LED 技术的最新进展已经能够产生各种波长的紫外线,每种波长都具有独特的特性。
核心方法
在这项研究中,我们评估了四种不同紫外线波长(222 nm、230 nm、254 nm 和 265 nm)灭活 HPAIV 的功效。用剂量范围为 0 至 7.3 mJ/cm2 的紫外线照射浓度为 2.0 × 106 噬菌斑形成单位 (PFU)/mL 的 HPAIV。
主要结果
对于 222 nm、230 nm、254 nm 和 265 nm 的紫外线波长,实现病毒滴度降低 99.9% 所需的紫外线剂量分别为 4.2 mJ、4.9 mJ、5.6 mJ 和 4.3 mJ。所有四种紫外线波长均可将病毒滴度降低 99.9% 以上,证明其灭活 HPAIV 的有效性。
解释与创新
这项研究表明,所有四个测试波长的紫外线都能在相当程度上有效地灭活 HPAIV,这表明紫外线可在多种环境中灭活 HPAIV。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持使用紫外线作为灭活 HPAIV 的实用且有效的方法,可应用于各种环境以降低 传播 和感染的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究为紫外线灭活 HPAIV 的功效提供了强有力的证据,但可能需要进一步的研究来评估在现实条件下和针对其他病毒株的有效性。

Academic paper (Japanese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Japanese)
Inactivation of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza Viruses Using Deep Ultraviolet Radiation of at Different Wavelengths 波長の異なる深紫外線による高病原性鳥インフルエンザウイルスの不活化
Authors: Ryosuke MATSUURA, Akatsuki SAITO, Fumihiro NAGATA, Noriko FUKUSHI, Raita HORIGUCHI, Kunio KANEMARU, Yuji ODA, Koichi MATSUO, Yoko AIDA
Original abstract (Japanese)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) of the H5N1 subtype have been reported to infect not only birds, but also humans, cattle, and other mammals, posing a serious public health concern and highlighting the need for effective control measures. Ultraviolet (UV) irradiation is widely known as an effective modality for virus inactivation, and recent advances in LED technology have enabled the generation of UV light of various wavelengths, each with distinct characteristics. In this study, we evaluated the efficacy of four different UV wavelengths (222 nm, 230 nm, 254 nm, and 265 nm) in inactivating HPAIVs. HPAIV at a concentration of 2.0 × 106 plaque-forming units (PFU) /mL was irradiated with UV light at doses ranging from 0 to 7.3 mJ/cm2. The UV doses required to achieve a 99.9% reduction in the viral titer were 4.2 mJ, 4.9 mJ, 5.6 mJ, and 4.3 mJ for UV light wavelengths of 222 nm, 230 nm, 254 nm, and 265 nm, respectively. All four UV light wavelengths reduced viral titers by more than 99.9%, demonstrating their effectiveness in inactivating HPAIVs. This study demonstrated that UV light at all the four tested wavelengths effectively inactivated HPAIVs to a comparable extent, suggesting the usefulness of UV light for inactivating HPAIVs in a wide range of environments.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) of the H5N1 subtype have been reported to infect not only birds, but also humans, cattle, and other mammals, posing a serious public health concern and highlighting the need for effective control measures.
Design and population
Ultraviolet (UV) irradiation is widely known as an effective modality for virus inactivation, and recent advances in LED technology have enabled the generation of UV light of various wavelengths, each with distinct characteristics.
Core methods
In this study, we evaluated the efficacy of four different UV wavelengths (222 nm, 230 nm, 254 nm, and 265 nm) in inactivating HPAIVs. HPAIV at a concentration of 2.0 × 106 plaque-forming units (PFU)/mL was irradiated with UV light at doses ranging from 0 to 7.3 mJ/cm2.
Main results
The UV doses required to achieve a 99.9% reduction in the viral titer were 4.2 mJ, 4.9 mJ, 5.6 mJ, and 4.3 mJ for UV light wavelengths of 222 nm, 230 nm, 254 nm, and 265 nm, respectively. All four UV light wavelengths reduced viral titers by more than 99.9%, demonstrating their effectiveness in inactivating HPAIVs.
Interpretation and novelty
This study demonstrated that UV light at all the four tested wavelengths effectively inactivated HPAIVs to a comparable extent, suggesting the usefulness of UV light for inactivating HPAIVs in a wide range of environments.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of UV light as a practical and effective method for inactivating HPAIVs, which could be applied in various settings to reduce the risk of transmission and infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study provides strong evidence for the efficacy of UV light in inactivating HPAIVs, but further research may be needed to evaluate the effectiveness in real-world conditions and against other viral strains.

单细胞和空间转录组分析揭示了感染 H1N1 与 H5N1 流感病毒的小鼠肺部的不同免疫景观

Single-cell and spatial transcriptomic profiling reveals distinct immune landscapes in murine lungs infected with H1N1 versus H5N1 influenza viruses
作者： Qianqian Zhang, Yuying Zhang, Hailiang Sun, Haoning Li, Yao Wang, Fanhua Wei
摘要中文翻译摘要 不同致病性的甲型流感病毒会引发不同的 宿主 免疫应答,但潜在的细胞和空间动力学仍不清楚。在这里,我们整合单细胞 RNA 测序 (scRNA-seq) 和空间转录组学,系统地比较感染季节性 H1N1 病毒或属于进化枝 2.3.2.1 的 高致病性禽流感 H5N1 病毒后小鼠肺部的免疫状况。对 84,162 个免疫细胞的分析确定了 12 种不同的细胞类型,表明 H1N1 感染与强烈的干扰素和炎症反应相关,其特征是肺泡巨噬细胞耗竭以及单核细胞和中性粒细胞广泛浸润。相比之下,H5N1 进化枝 2.3.2.1 感染的特征是干扰素信号减弱、单核细胞激活延迟、中性粒细胞成熟失调和细胞间通讯受损,这些特征可能与之前表征的 H5N1 毒株不同。空间映射表明两种感染之间免疫细胞的不同分布和相互作用模式。通过骨髓细胞耗竭进行的功能验证支持单核细胞-巨噬细胞轴在疾病进展中的关键作用。这些发现为不同致病性的流感病毒提供了肺部 免疫应答 的高分辨率图谱,并强调了免疫调节的细胞类型特异性机制。重要性 季节性 H1N1 流感病毒导致每年流行,而高致病性禽 H5N1 病毒具有高 死亡率 率和大流行潜力。了解 H5N1 为何会导致更严重的疾病对于开发更好的 治疗 至关重要。在这项研究中,我们使用先进的单细胞和空间技术创建了感染 H1N1 或 clade 2.3.2.1 H5N1 病毒的小鼠肺部 免疫应答 的详细图谱。我们发现 H1N1 会触发一个强大的、组织良好的 免疫应答 来控制病毒。相比之下,H5N1 感染会导致反应混乱和减弱,关键免疫细胞无法激活和正常通讯。这些发现表明 H5N1 病毒可能逃避并抑制 宿主 的免疫系统。我们的研究提供了高分辨率的免疫图谱,并确定了针对严重流感的新疗法的潜在靶标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
不同致病性的甲型流感病毒会引发不同的 宿主 免疫应答,但潜在的细胞和空间动力学仍不清楚。了解 H5N1 为何会导致更严重的疾病对于开发更好的 治疗 至关重要。
设计与对象
在这里,我们整合单细胞 RNA 测序 (scRNA-seq) 和空间转录组学,系统地比较感染季节性 H1N1 或 高致病性禽流感 H5N1 病毒后小鼠肺部的免疫状况。该研究的重点是感染 H1N1 病毒或 clade 2.3.2.1 H5N1 病毒的小鼠。
核心方法
在这项研究中,我们使用先进的单细胞和空间技术创建了感染 H1N1 或 clade 2.3.2.1 H5N1 病毒的小鼠肺部 免疫应答 的详细图谱。对 84,162 个免疫细胞的分析确定了 12 种不同的细胞类型。
主要结果
相比之下,H5N1 进化枝 2.3.2.1 感染的特点是干扰素信号减弱、单核细胞激活延迟、中性粒细胞成熟失调和细胞间通讯受损。
解释与创新
这些发现表明 H5N1 病毒可能逃避并抑制 宿主 的免疫系统。这些发现为不同致病性的流感病毒提供了肺部 免疫应答 的高分辨率图谱,并强调了免疫调节的细胞类型特异性机制。
科研与公卫意义
我们的研究提供了高分辨率的免疫图谱,并确定了针对严重流感的新疗法的潜在靶标。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性。

Single-cell and spatial transcriptomic profiling reveals distinct immune landscapes in murine lungs infected with H1N1 versus H5N1 influenza viruses

Authors: Qianqian Zhang, Yuying Zhang, Hailiang Sun, Haoning Li, Yao Wang, Fanhua Wei
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Influenza A viruses of divergent pathogenicity elicit distinct host immune responses, yet the underlying cellular and spatial dynamics remain poorly defined. Here, we integrate single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) and spatial transcriptomics to systematically compare the immune landscapes in murine lungs following infection with seasonal H1N1 or a highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1 virus belonging to clade 2.3.2.1. Analysis of 84,162 immune cells identifies 12 distinct cell types, suggesting that H1N1 infection is associated with robust interferon and inflammatory responses, characterized by alveolar macrophage depletion and extensive infiltration of monocytes and neutrophils. In contrast, H5N1 clade 2.3.2.1 infection is characterized by attenuated interferon signaling, delayed monocyte activation, dysregulated neutrophil maturation, and impaired intercellular communication—features that may be distinct from previously characterized H5N1 strains. Spatial mapping indicates distinct distribution and interaction patterns of immune cells between the two infections. Functional validation through myeloid cell depletion supports the critical role of the monocyte-macrophage axis in disease progression. These findings provide a high-resolution atlas of pulmonary immune responses to influenza viruses of differing pathogenicity and highlight cell-type-specific mechanisms of immune modulation. IMPORTANCE Seasonal H1N1 influenza virus causes annual epidemics, while highly pathogenic avian H5N1 virus has a high mortality rate and pandemic potential. Understanding why H5N1 causes more severe disease is critical for developing better treatments. In this study, we used advanced single-cell and spatial technologies to create a detailed map of the immune response in the lungs of mice infected with either H1N1 or a clade 2.3.2.1 H5N1 virus. We discovered that H1N1 triggers a strong, well-organized immune response that controls the virus. In contrast, H5N1 infection leads to a disorganized and weakened response where key immune cells fail to activate and communicate properly. These findings suggest that the H5N1 virus may evade and suppress the host’s immune system. Our study provides a high-resolution immune atlas and identifies potential targets for new therapies against severe influenza.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A viruses of divergent pathogenicity elicit distinct host immune responses, yet the underlying cellular and spatial dynamics remain poorly defined. Understanding why H5N1 causes more severe disease is critical for developing better treatments.
Design and population

The study focuses on mice infected with either H1N1 or a clade 2.3.2.1 H5N1 virus.
Core methods
In this study, we used advanced single-cell and spatial technologies to create a detailed map of the immune response in the lungs of mice infected with either H1N1 or a clade 2.3.2.1 H5N1 virus. Analysis of 84,162 immune cells identifies 12 distinct cell types.
Main results
In contrast, H5N1 clade 2.3.2.1 infection is characterized by attenuated interferon signaling, delayed monocyte activation, dysregulated neutrophil maturation, and impaired intercellular communication.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that the H5N1 virus may evade and suppress the host’s immune system. These findings provide a high-resolution atlas of pulmonary immune responses to influenza viruses of differing pathogenicity and highlight cell-type-specific mechanisms of immune modulation.
Scientific and public-health significance
Our study provides a high-resolution immune atlas and identifies potential targets for new therapies against severe influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

冲突阻碍了对动物运动的研究。

Conflicts hinder research into animal movements.
作者： Víctor Martín-Vélez, Joan Navarro, Isabel Afán, Tomas Montalvo, Andy J Green
摘要中文翻译卫星追踪彻底改变了我们对动物迁徙的理解,但其可靠性越来越依赖于野生动物经常出没地区的地缘政治稳定性。在这里,我们表明,在东欧持续冲突期间,军事对全球导航卫星系统(GNSS)的干扰严重影响了基于全球定位系统(GPS)的黑头鸥(Chroicocephalus rodibundus)跟踪数据的准确性。2024-2025 年,GPS 轨迹显示出不稳定、低质量且在地理上不可信的位置,与已知的电子战区域重合。这些不准确之处阻碍了定位繁殖群、确定关键中途停留栖息地以及评估候鸟带来的疾病 传播 风险的工作,特别是 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 等人畜共患病的风险。我们的研究结果说明了现代冲突如何将其影响扩展到生态研究基础设施,呼吁采取系统的纠正方法和国际协调,以在地缘政治不稳定的世界中保障运动生态学研究和同一个健康模型的稳健性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
卫星追踪彻底改变了我们对动物迁徙的理解,但其可靠性越来越依赖于野生动物经常出没地区的地缘政治稳定性。
设计与对象
在这里,我们表明,在东欧持续冲突期间,军事对全球导航卫星系统(GNSS)的干扰严重影响了基于全球定位系统(GPS)的黑头鸥(Chroicocephalus rodibundus)跟踪数据的准确性。
核心方法
2024-2025 年,GPS 轨迹显示出不稳定、低质量且在地理上不可信的位置,与已知的电子战区域重合。
主要结果
这些不准确之处阻碍了定位繁殖群、确定关键中途停留栖息地以及评估候鸟带来的疾病 传播 风险的工作,特别是 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 等人畜共患病的风险。
解释与创新
我们的研究结果说明了现代冲突如何将其影响扩展到生态研究基础设施,呼吁采取系统的纠正方法和国际协调,以在地缘政治不稳定的世界中保障运动生态学研究和同一个健康模型的稳健性。
科研与公卫意义
受损的跟踪数据影响了关键的生态和公共卫生研究,包括对候鸟疾病 传播 风险的评估,特别是 HPAI H5N1 等人畜共患病的风险。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于特定冲突地区的 GPS 轨迹分析,限制了对其他地区或时间段的普遍适用性;然而,对干扰的直接观察为报告的影响提供了有力的证据。

Conflicts hinder research into animal movements.

Authors: Víctor Martín-Vélez, Joan Navarro, Isabel Afán, Tomas Montalvo, Andy J Green
Original abstract (English)
Satellite tracking has revolutionized our understanding of animal migration, yet its reliability increasingly depends on the geopolitical stability of the regions frequented by wildlife. Here, we show that military-induced interference with global navigation satellite systems (GNSS) during ongoing conflicts in Eastern Europe has severely compromised the accuracy of global positioning systems (GPS)-based tracking data for black-headed gulls (Chroicocephalus ridibundus). In 2024-2025, GPS trajectories revealed erratic, low-quality, and geographically implausible positions coinciding with known zones of electronic warfare. These inaccuracies hinder efforts to locate breeding colonies, identify key stopover habitats, and assess disease transmission risks posed by migratory birds, particularly for zoonoses such as highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1. Our findings illustrate how modern conflicts now extend their impact into ecological research infrastructures, calling for systematic correction methods and international coordination to safeguard the robustness of movement ecology studies and One Health models in a geopolitically unstable world.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Satellite tracking has revolutionized our understanding of animal migration, yet its reliability increasingly depends on the geopolitical stability of the regions frequented by wildlife.
Design and population
Here, we show that military-induced interference with global navigation satellite systems (GNSS) during ongoing conflicts in Eastern Europe has severely compromised the accuracy of global positioning systems (GPS)-based tracking data for black-headed gulls (Chroicocephalus ridibundus).
Core methods
In 2024-2025, GPS trajectories revealed erratic, low-quality, and geographically implausible positions coinciding with known zones of electronic warfare.
Main results
These inaccuracies hinder efforts to locate breeding colonies, identify key stopover habitats, and assess disease transmission risks posed by migratory birds, particularly for zoonoses such as highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1.
Interpretation and novelty
Our findings illustrate how modern conflicts now extend their impact into ecological research infrastructures, calling for systematic correction methods and international coordination to safeguard the robustness of movement ecology studies and One Health models in a geopolitically unstable world.
Scientific and public-health significance
The compromised tracking data affects critical ecological and public health research, including the assessment of disease transmission risks by migratory birds, particularly for zoonoses like HPAI H5N1.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on GPS trajectory analysis during specific conflict zones, limiting generalizability to other regions or time periods; however, the direct observation of interference provides strong evidence for the reported impact.

VSV-媒介 疫苗 同时靶向 H5N1 血凝素和基质蛋白 2,可诱导强大的中和和 ADCC 抗体反应,并在小鼠模型中提供针对致命 H5N1 感染的全面保护

A VSV-vector vaccine simultaneously targeting H5N1 hemagglutinin and matrix protein 2 induces robust neutralizing and ADCC antibody responses and provides full protection against lethal H5N1 infection in a mouse model
作者： Zhujun Ao, Robert Vendramelli, Maryann Buyu, Thang Truong, Dayu Liu, Nick Gao, Heqing Ma, Stephen Amos, Peter Lawrynuik, Maurice Nyamekye
摘要中文翻译摘要 禽流感 A 病毒,尤其是 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 病毒,对公共卫生构成重大威胁,多价 疫苗 是控制这些病毒的主要预防措施。为了建立这样的疫苗,我们生成了两种基于多价水泡性口炎病毒(VSV)的疫苗候选物,它们同时靶向H5N1血凝素(H5 05或H5 22)和基质蛋白2(M2e)的胞外域,并表征了它们诱导保护性免疫应答的能力。我们的结果表明,疫苗 免疫诱导小鼠体内针对 HPAI 血凝素 (HA) 蛋白和 M2 蛋白的高体液 免疫应答。有趣的是,疫苗 免疫的小鼠血清对相应的 H5 假病毒表现出高效的中和活性,并对源自人类和 禽流感 H5、H1、H3 和 H7 病毒的 M2e 介导有效且广泛的抗体依赖性细胞毒性 (ADCC) 活性。此外,V-EtM2e/H5 22 的鼻内 (IN) 和肌内免疫 (IM) 均可有效保护小鼠免受 HPAI H5N1 病毒的攻击,存活率达 100%,并且在多个组织中病毒载量不可检测。值得注意的是,非侵入性粘膜 IN 递送以低 100 倍的免疫剂量实现了与 IM 递送相同的保护。这些发现为基于多价 VSV 的 疫苗 对抗人(禽)甲型流感病毒的有效性提供了强有力的证据。重要性 禽流感,尤其是 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 病毒,可引起高度传染性的空气传播疾病,对公共健康构成重大威胁。多价 疫苗 的开发对于控制这些病毒感染非常重要。在这项研究中,我们开发了一种多价 疫苗,它同时针对两种关键的 HPAI H5N1 表面病毒蛋白,并在小鼠模型中完全防止高致病性 H5N1 感染。这项研究为多价 疫苗 对抗高致病性甲型流感病毒的有效性提供了令人信服的证据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 A 病毒,尤其是 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 病毒,对公共卫生构成重大威胁,多价 疫苗 是控制这些病毒的主要预防措施。
设计与对象
为了建立这样的疫苗,我们生成了两种基于多价水泡性口炎病毒(VSV)的疫苗候选物,它们同时靶向H5N1血凝素(H5 05或H5 22)和基质蛋白2(M2e)的胞外域,并表征了它们在小鼠中诱导保护性免疫应答的能力。
核心方法
我们的结果表明,疫苗 免疫诱导小鼠体内针对 HPAI 血凝素 (HA) 蛋白和 M2 蛋白的高体液 免疫应答。
主要结果
有趣的是,疫苗 免疫的小鼠血清对相应的 H5 假病毒表现出高效的中和活性,并对源自人类和 禽流感 H5、H1、H3 和 H7 病毒的 M2e 介导有效且广泛的抗体依赖性细胞毒性 (ADCC) 活性。此外,V-EtM2e/H5 22 的鼻内 (IN) 和肌内免疫 (IM) 均可有效保护小鼠免受 HPAI H5N1 病毒的攻击,存活率达 100%,并且在多个组织中病毒载量不可检测。
解释与创新
值得注意的是,非侵入性粘膜 IN 递送以低 100 倍的免疫剂量实现了与 IM 递送相同的保护,为基于多价 VSV 的 疫苗 对抗人(禽)甲型流感病毒的有效性提供了有力的证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为多价 疫苗 对抗高致病性甲型流感病毒的有效性提供了令人信服的证据,突出了其作为控制 HPAI 感染的预防措施的潜力。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在小鼠模型中进行的,虽然结果令人鼓舞,但还需要进一步的研究来评估 疫苗 在其他动物模型和人类中的功效。由于 免疫应答 的稳健性和在实验环境中观察到的全面保护,证据强度很高。

A VSV-vector vaccine simultaneously targeting H5N1 hemagglutinin and matrix protein 2 induces robust neutralizing and ADCC antibody responses and provides full protection against lethal H5N1 infection in a mouse model

Authors: Zhujun Ao, Robert Vendramelli, Maryann Buyu, Thang Truong, Dayu Liu, Nick Gao, Heqing Ma, Stephen Amos, Peter Lawrynuik, Maurice Nyamekye
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Avian influenza A viruses, especially highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) viruses, pose a significant public health threat, and a multivalent vaccine is the primary prophylactic measure to control these viruses. To establish such a vaccine, we generated two multivalent vesicular stomatitis virus (VSV)-based vaccine candidates that simultaneously target H5N1 hemagglutinin (H5 05 or H5 22 ) and the ectodomain of matrix protein 2 (M2e) and characterized their ability to induce protective immune responses. Our results revealed that vaccine immunization induced high humoral immune responses against both the HPAI hemagglutinin (HA) protein and the M2 protein in mice. Intriguingly, vaccine-immunized mouse sera exhibited highly efficient neutralizing activity against the corresponding H5 pseudovirus and mediated potent and broad antibody-dependent cellular cytotoxicity (ADCC) activity against M2e derived from human and avian influenza H5, H1, H3, and H7 viruses. Furthermore, both intranasal (IN) and intramuscular immunization (IM) of V-EtM2e/H5 22 provided efficient protection against HPAI H5N1 virus challenge in mice, with a 100% survival rate and a nondetectable viral load in several tissues. Notably, noninvasive mucosal IN delivery achieved protection equal to that of IM delivery at a 100-fold lower immunizing dose. These findings provide strong evidence for the effectiveness of a multivalent VSV-based vaccine against human (avian) influenza A viruses. IMPORTANCE Avian influenza, especially highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) viruses, can cause a highly contagious airborne disease that poses a significant public health threat. The development of a multivalent vaccine is very important to control these viral infections. In this study, we have developed a multivalent vaccine that simultaneously targets two critical HPAI H5N1 surface viral proteins and fully protects against highly pathogenic H5N1 infection in a mouse model. This study provides convincing evidence for the effectiveness of a multivalent vaccine against highly pathogenic influenza A viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza A viruses, especially highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) viruses, pose a significant public health threat, and a multivalent vaccine is the primary prophylactic measure to control these viruses.
Design and population
To establish such a vaccine, we generated two multivalent vesicular stomatitis virus (VSV)-based vaccine candidates that simultaneously target H5N1 hemagglutinin (H5 05 or H5 22) and the ectodomain of matrix protein 2 (M2e) and characterized their ability to induce protective immune responses in mice.
Core methods
Our results revealed that vaccine immunization induced high humoral immune responses against both the HPAI hemagglutinin (HA) protein and the M2 protein in mice.
Main results
Intriguingly, vaccine-immunized mouse sera exhibited highly efficient neutralizing activity against the corresponding H5 pseudovirus and mediated potent and broad antibody-dependent cellular cytotoxicity (ADCC) activity against M2e derived from human and avian influenza H5, H1, H3, and H7 viruses. Furthermore, both intranasal (IN) and intramuscular immunization (IM) of V-EtM2e/H5 22 provided efficient protection against HPAI H5N1 virus challenge in mice, with a 100% survival rate and a nondetectable viral load in several tissues.
Interpretation and novelty
Notably, noninvasive mucosal IN delivery achieved protection equal to that of IM delivery at a 100-fold lower immunizing dose, providing strong evidence for the effectiveness of a multivalent VSV-based vaccine against human (avian) influenza A viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide convincing evidence for the effectiveness of a multivalent vaccine against highly pathogenic influenza A viruses, highlighting its potential as a prophylactic measure to control HPAI infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a mouse model, and while the results are promising, further research is needed to evaluate the vaccine's efficacy in other animal models and humans. The evidence strength is high due to the robust immune responses and full protection observed in the experimental setting.
