酸、pH 值和孵育时间对 禽流感 病毒在原奶中持久性的影响

The impact of acids, pH, and incubation time on avian influenza virus persistence in raw milk
作者： Harrell TL, Shwani A, Suarez DL
摘要中文翻译2024 年 3 月,高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 进化枝 2.3.4.4。b 在奶牛中检测到 H5N1。自检测以来,该病毒已在 17 个州传播,感染了 1000 多个奶牛群,引起了乳制品行业对其对奶牛的影响、与牛奶质量和生产相关的风险以及最终对人类的风险的担忧。研究表明,巴氏灭菌足以灭活牛奶中存在的 HPAIV,使其可供人类安全食用。然而,经常食用并用于制作奶酪、酸奶和开菲尔的未经巴氏消毒的生奶是一个公共健康问题。我们用乙酸、丙酸、乳酸或柠檬酸酸化原料奶,这一过程类似于生产发酵乳产品的初始阶段,将 pH 值调至 5;或用乙酸将其酸化至 pH 6、5 或 4。随后将每个样品掺入低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV),并在室温下孵育长达 24 小时,然后接种到 10 日龄的无特定病原体的胚胎鸡蛋中。每天评估胚胎的活力,并使用尿囊液的血凝测定来确认存活的 LPAIV 的存在。活病毒的减少与时间和 pH 值显着相关,但与测试的特定酸类型无关。pH 4 和 5 的酸 治疗 随着时间的推移逐渐降低活 LPAIV 水平,在 24 小时观察到最明显的失活。pH 值为 6 的样品病毒活力几乎没有降低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 分支 2.3.4.4b H5N1 在 17 个州的奶牛中传播,引发了人们对未经高温消毒的原料奶的牛奶质量、生产和人类健康风险的担忧,因此需要进行这项研究。
设计与对象
该研究涉及用各种酸将生奶酸化至不同的 pH 值,添加低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV),并在室温下孵育长达 24 小时,然后接种到 10 日龄的无特定病原体的胚胎鸡蛋中。
核心方法
将样品用乙酸、丙酸、乳酸或柠檬酸酸化至 pH 5,或用乙酸酸化至 pH 6、5 或 4,加入 LPAIV,孵育,然后接种到含胚鸡蛋中,每天通过血凝测定评估活力。
主要结果
活病毒的减少与时间和 pH 值显着相关,但与测试的酸的具体类型无关;pH 6 的样品显示病毒活力几乎没有降低,而 pH 4 和 5 的酸 治疗 随着时间的推移逐渐降低活的 LPAIV 水平。
解释与创新
作者解释说,酸 治疗,特别是在较低 pH 水平下,可以有效灭活原料奶中的 LPAIV,为降低与未经巴氏消毒的乳制品相关的公共卫生风险提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为减少原奶中 禽流感 病毒持久性的潜在干预措施提供了信息,从而减轻发酵食品中使用的未经高温消毒的乳制品对人类健康的风险。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究是在受控实验室环境中使用胚胎鸡蛋进行的,这可能无法完全复制乳制品加工或人类消费的现实条件。

The impact of acids, pH, and incubation time on avian influenza virus persistence in raw milk

Authors: Harrell TL, Shwani A, Suarez DL
Original abstract (English)
In March 2024, highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) clade 2.3.4.4. b H5N1 was detected in dairy cattle. Since detection, the virus has spread across 17 states, infecting more than 1000 dairy herds, causing concern for the dairy industry regarding the effects on dairy cattle, the risk associated with milk quality and production, and ultimately its risk to humans. It has been shown that pasteurization is sufficient to inactivate HPAIV, if present in milk, making it safe for human consumption. However, unpasteurized raw milk that is routinely consumed and used to make cheese, yogurt, and kefir is a public health concern. We acidified raw milk, a process that is analogous to the preliminary phase of generating fermented milk products, with acetic, propionic, lactic, or citric acid to pH 5; or acidified it with acetic acid to pH 6, 5, or 4. Each sample was subsequently spiked with low pathogenic avian influenza virus (LPAIV) and incubated at room temperature for up to 24 h before being inoculated into 10-day old specific-pathogen-free embryonating chicken eggs. Embryos were assessed daily for viability and hemagglutination assays on the allantoic fluid was used to confirm the presence of viable LPAIV. The reduction of viable virus was significantly correlated with both time and pH but not specific type of acid tested. An acid treatment at pH 4 and 5 progressively reduced viable LPAIV levels over time, with the most pronounced inactivation observed at 24 h. Samples at pH 6 had little reduction in virus viability.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed due to the spread of highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 in dairy cattle across 17 states, raising concerns about milk quality, production, and human health risks from unpasteurized raw milk.
Design and population
The study involved acidifying raw milk with various acids to different pH levels, spiking it with low pathogenic avian influenza virus (LPAIV), and incubating it at room temperature for up to 24 hours before inoculating into 10-day-old specific-pathogen-free embryonating chicken eggs.
Core methods
Samples were acidified with acetic, propionic, lactic, or citric acid to pH 5 or with acetic acid to pH 6, 5, or 4, spiked with LPAIV, incubated, and then inoculated into embryonating chicken eggs, with viability assessed daily via hemagglutination assays.
Main results
The reduction of viable virus was significantly correlated with both time and pH but not the specific type of acid tested; samples at pH 6 showed little reduction in virus viability, while acid treatment at pH 4 and 5 progressively reduced viable LPAIV levels over time.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that acid treatment, particularly at lower pH levels, can effectively inactivate LPAIV in raw milk, providing a novel approach to reduce public health risks associated with unpasteurized dairy products.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform potential interventions for reducing avian influenza virus persistence in raw milk, thereby mitigating risks to human health from unpasteurized dairy products used in fermented foods.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study was conducted in a controlled laboratory setting using embryonating chicken eggs, which may not fully replicate real-world conditions in dairy processing or human consumption.
来源来源
Journal: Journal of Applied Data Sciences  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-20  |  DOI: 10.47738/jads.v7i3.1416  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 7, 3, 1943-1963
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的牛顿差分优化

Newton Divided Difference Optimization for Fingerprint-Based Neural Virtual Screening against Avian Influenza A/H9N2
作者： Siti Amiroch
摘要中文翻译禽流感 A/H9N2 造成持续的人畜共患病和兽医威胁,但用于 抗病毒药物 化合物优先级排序的有效计算工具仍然不发达,特别是在高维神经筛选中的​​优化器行为方面。本研究提出了牛顿除差(NDD)优化,这是一种轻量级正对角曲率感知训练策略,作为针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的新型优化器,目的是评估其在对齐的分子指纹表示和化学结构验证协议中的性能。由 615 种候选活性化合物和 844 种诱饵组成的 1,459 个分子的对齐基准由 EState(79 个特征)、PubChem(881 个特征)和 Klekota–Roth(4,860 个特征)指纹代表,共享相同的分子身份、标签和分割分配。固定多层感知器 (MLP) 分类器使用 NDD 和七个基线优化器在分层、支架键和相似性聚类分割协议下跨五个重复种子进行训练。NDD 在支架密钥和相似性聚类协议下的 Klekota–Roth 指纹上实现了最高的描述性 ROC-AUC(接收者操作特征 - 曲线下面积)和 PR-AUC(曲线下精确召回面积),并且在分层分割下保持竞争力,ROC-AUC 为 0.9872。架构敏感性测试证实了跨多种网络配置的稳定 NDD 性能,ROC-AUC 值范围为 0.9876 至 0.9890。与无 Hessian 优化相比,在相同的仅 CPU 配置下,NDD 将 Klekota-Roth 上的每次运行时间从大约 50-54 秒减少到大约 8 秒,同时实现了相当的排名性能。这项工作的新颖性在于首次系统评估 H9N2 神经虚拟筛选的 NDD,证明正对角线曲率感知缩放在稀疏高维配体筛选设置中提供了实用、稳定且计算高效的优化替代方案,尽管在实际 抗病毒药物 优先级排序之前仍然需要外部验证和前瞻性实验确认。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 A/H9N2 造成持续的人畜共患病和兽医威胁,但用于 抗病毒药物 化合物优先级排序的有效计算工具仍然不发达,特别是在高维神经筛选中的​​优化器行为方面。
设计与对象
本研究提出牛顿差分(NDD)优化作为一种​​针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的新型优化器,目的是评估其在对齐的分子指纹表示和化学结构验证协议中的性能。
核心方法
由 615 种候选活性化合物和 844 种诱饵组成的 1,459 个分子的对齐基准由 EState(79 个特征)、PubChem(881 个特征)和 Klekota–Roth(4,860 个特征)指纹代表,共享相同的分子身份、标签和分割分配。固定多层感知器 (MLP) 分类器使用 NDD 和七个基线优化器在分层、支架键和相似性聚类分割协议下跨五个重复种子进行训练。
主要结果
NDD 在支架关键和相似性聚类协议下在 Klekota–Roth 指纹上实现了最高的描述性 ROC-AUC 和 PR-AUC,并且在分层分割下保持竞争力,ROC-AUC 为 0.9872。架构敏感性测试证实了跨多种网络配置的稳定 NDD 性能,ROC-AUC 值范围为 0.9876 至 0.9890。与无 Hessian 优化相比,在相同的仅 CPU 配置下,NDD 将 Klekota-Roth 上的每次运行时间从大约 50-54 秒减少到大约 8 秒,同时实现了相当的排名性能。
解释与创新
这项工作的新颖性在于首次对 H9N2 神经虚拟筛选的 NDD 进行系统评估,证明正对角线曲率感知缩放在基于稀疏高维配体的筛选设置中提供了实用、稳定且计算高效的优化替代方案。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,NDD 优化可以显着加速针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的 抗病毒药物 化合物的虚拟筛选,而不会影响性能,从而有可能更快地确定候选药物的优先级,以进行进一步的实验验证。
局限与证据强度
在实施实际的 抗病毒药物 优先级排序之前,外部验证和前瞻性实验确认仍然是必要的。由于该研究的计算性质和缺乏实验验证,证据强度中等。

Newton Divided Difference Optimization for Fingerprint-Based Neural Virtual Screening against Avian Influenza A/H9N2

Authors: Siti Amiroch
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza A/H9N2 poses persistent zoonotic and veterinary threats, yet efficient computational tools for antiviral compound prioritization remain underdeveloped, particularly with respect to optimizer behavior in high-dimensional neural screening. This study proposes Newton Divided Difference (NDD) optimization, a lightweight positive diagonal curvature-aware training strategy, as a novel optimizer for fingerprint-based neural virtual screening against avian influenza A/H9N2, with the objective of evaluating its performance across aligned molecular fingerprint representations and chemically structured validation protocols. An aligned benchmark of 1,459 molecules consisting of 615 candidate active compounds and 844 decoys was represented by EState (79 features), PubChem (881 features), and Klekota–Roth (4,860 features) fingerprints, sharing identical molecule identities, labels, and split assignments. A fixed multilayer perceptron (MLP) classifier was trained with NDD and seven baseline optimizers under stratified, scaffold-key, and similarity-cluster split protocols across five repeated seeds. NDD achieved the highest descriptive ROC-AUC (Receiver Operating Characteriztic – Area Under the Curve) and PR-AUC (Precision-Recall Area Under the Curve) on Klekota–Roth fingerprints under scaffold-key and similarity-cluster protocols, and remained competitive under the stratified split with ROC-AUC of 0.9872. Architecture-sensitivity tests confirmed stable NDD performance across multiple network configurations, with ROC-AUC values ranging from 0.9876 to 0.9890. Compared with Hessian-free optimization, NDD reduced per-run runtime from approximately 50–54 seconds to approximately 8 seconds on Klekota–Roth under the same CPU-only configuration while achieving comparable ranking performance. The novelty of this work lies in the first systematic assessment of NDD for H9N2 neural virtual screening, demonstrating that positive diagonal curvature-aware scaling provides a practical, stable, and computationally efficient optimization alternative in sparse high-dimensional ligand-based screening settings, although external validation and prospective experimental confirmation remain necessary before practical antiviral prioritization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza A/H9N2 poses persistent zoonotic and veterinary threats, yet efficient computational tools for antiviral compound prioritization remain underdeveloped, particularly with respect to optimizer behavior in high-dimensional neural screening.
Design and population
This study proposes Newton Divided Difference (NDD) optimization as a novel optimizer for fingerprint-based neural virtual screening against avian influenza A/H9N2, with the objective of evaluating its performance across aligned molecular fingerprint representations and chemically structured validation protocols.

Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that NDD optimization can significantly accelerate virtual screening for antiviral compounds against avian influenza A/H9N2 without compromising performance, potentially enabling faster prioritization of candidate drugs for further experimental validation.
Limitations and evidence strength
External validation and prospective experimental confirmation remain necessary before practical antiviral prioritization can be implemented. The evidence strength is moderate due to the computational nature of the study and the lack of experimental validation.

亚型特异性中和抗体在流感病毒共感染期间促进抗原转变。

Subtype-specific neutralizing antibodies promote antigenic shift during influenza virus co-infection.
作者： Mengnan Liu, Xiaoran Gao, Xueyang Li, Jinyang Hao, Christine Light, Yu Tian, Siqin Wang, Wenyu Yan, Rong Hai, Wei Hu
摘要中文翻译当不同流感毒株共同感染相同的 宿主 细胞时,就会发生不同流感毒株之间的重配。重配病毒的出现取决于其内在适应性和外在因素,包括预先存在的体液免疫。鸟类中 H2N2 和 H3N2 等大流行毒株以及 H5N6、H5N8 和 H7N9 等人畜共患甲型流感病毒的产生被认为是先前存在的抗体广泛选择的结果。为了进一步探讨体液免疫在重配中的作用,我们生成了两种不同的表达荧光蛋白的病毒,并使用毒株特异性和交叉反应性单克隆抗体(mAb)来评估交叉免疫对重配的影响。我们的结果表明,所有单克隆抗体都改变了基因型多样性,并在体外和体内显着减少了共感染细胞中子代病毒粒子的释放。此外,小鼠体内的抗体转移研究揭示了针对不同致病性特征的攻击的保护作用。值得注意的是,菌株特异性 mAb 驱动的选择取决于其中和特异性,而广泛反应性 mAb 驱动的选择则独立于中和特异性。我们的研究结果表明,预先存在的中和抗体会形成重配,并且毒株特异性中和抗体会促进共感染期间的抗原转变,而识别不同亚型流感病毒的广泛交叉反应抗体则并非如此。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查预先存在的体液免疫如何影响共同感染期间流感病毒的重排,特别是在抗原转变的情况下。背景强调,当不同流感毒株共同感染同一个 宿主 细胞时,它们之间就会发生重配,并且重配病毒的出现取决于内在适应性和外在因素,例如先前存在的免疫力。
设计与对象
该研究涉及生成两种不同的表达荧光蛋白的病毒,并使用单克隆抗体来评估交叉免疫对重配的影响。该研究在体外和体内进行,小鼠用于抗体转移研究,以评估针对不同致病性特征的保护作用。
核心方法
为了探索体液免疫在重配中的作用,研究人员创建了两种不同的荧光病毒,并应用了毒株特异性和交叉反应的单克隆抗体。他们在体外和体内评估了这些抗体对共感染细胞的基因型多样性和子代病毒粒子释放的影响。
主要结果
结果表明,所有单克隆抗体都改变了基因型多样性,并显着减少了共感染细胞中子代病毒粒子的释放。此外,小鼠中的抗体转移研究表明,可以保护免受不同致病性特征的攻击。
解释与创新
作者解释说,预先存在的中和抗体会影响重配,与识别不同亚型的广泛交叉反应抗体不同,菌株特异性抗体在共感染期间促进抗原转变。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,了解中和抗体在重配中的作用可以为 监测 和 禽流感 的预防策略提供信息,特别是在共感染和抗原转变相关的情况下。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和体内模型,证据仅基于抽象数据。这些结果得到了对共感染细胞的直接观察的支持,为重配过程中抗体驱动选择的解释提供了适度的信心。

Subtype-specific neutralizing antibodies promote antigenic shift during influenza virus co-infection.

Authors: Mengnan Liu, Xiaoran Gao, Xueyang Li, Jinyang Hao, Christine Light, Yu Tian, Siqin Wang, Wenyu Yan, Rong Hai, Wei Hu
Original abstract (English)
Reassortment between different influenza strains occurs when they co-infect the same host cell. The emergence of a reassortant virus depends on both its intrinsic fitness and extrinsic factors, including preexisting humoral immunity. The generation of pandemic strains, such as H2N2 and H3N2, and zoonotic influenza A viruses, like H5N6, H5N8, and H7N9, in birds is suggested to be the result of extensive selection by preexisting antibodies. To further explore the role of humoral immunity in reassortment, we generated two divergent fluorescent protein-expressing viruses and used strain-specific and cross-reactive monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) to assess the impact of cross-immunity on reassortment. Our results indicate that all mAbs altered the genotypic diversity and significantly reduced the release of progeny virions in co-infected cells both in vitro and in vivo . Moreover, antibody transfer studies in mice revealed protection from challenge with divergent pathogenicity profiles. Notably, selection driven by a strain-specific mAb depended on its neutralizing specificity, whereas the selection driven by broadly reactive mAbs was independent of neutralization specificity. Our findings demonstrate that preexisting neutralizing antibodies shape reassortment and that strain-specific neutralizing antibodies promote antigenic shift during co-infection, which is not the case for broadly cross-reactive antibodies that recognize influenza viruses from different subtypes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how preexisting humoral immunity influences the reassortment of influenza viruses during co-infection, particularly in the context of antigenic shift. The background highlights that reassortment between different influenza strains occurs when they co-infect the same host cell, and the emergence of reassortant viruses depends on both intrinsic fitness and extrinsic factors like preexisting immunity.
Design and population
The study involved generating two divergent fluorescent protein-expressing viruses and using monoclonal antibodies to assess the impact of cross-immunity on reassortment. It was conducted in vitro and in vivo, with mice used for antibody transfer studies to evaluate protection against divergent pathogenicity profiles.
Core methods
To explore the role of humoral immunity in reassortment, the researchers created two divergent fluorescent viruses and applied strain-specific and cross-reactive monoclonal antibodies. They assessed the impact of these antibodies on genotypic diversity and progeny virion release in co-infected cells, both in vitro and in vivo.
Main results
The results showed that all monoclonal antibodies altered genotypic diversity and significantly reduced the release of progeny virions in co-infected cells. Additionally, antibody transfer studies in mice demonstrated protection from challenge with divergent pathogenicity profiles.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that preexisting neutralizing antibodies influence reassortment, with strain-specific antibodies promoting antigenic shift during co-infection, unlike broadly cross-reactive antibodies that recognize different subtypes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that understanding the role of neutralizing antibodies in reassortment could inform surveillance and prevention strategies for avian influenza, particularly in contexts where co-infection and antigenic shift are relevant.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and in vivo models, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data. The results are supported by direct observations in co-infected cells, providing moderate confidence in the interpretation of antibody-driven selection during reassortment.

针对 HA1 上保守的横向补丁的中和纳米抗体可提供针对多种 H7 禽流感 病毒的保护

A neutralizing nanobody targeting a conserved lateral patch on HA1 confers protection against multiple H7 avian influenza viruses
作者： Siqi Xu, Qinying Zhang, Xueer Xie, Mengruo Zhou, Yunxia Chen, Yutong Liu, Chenying Luo, Han Zheng, Saixiang Feng, Ming Liao
摘要中文翻译摘要 全球范围内已有人类感染 H7 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 的记录,涉及多个亚型和地理区域。然而,针对 H7 流感病毒的有效疗法仍然有限。在这里,通过酵母双杂交 (Y2H) 筛选鉴定了一组靶向血凝素 (HA) HA1 结构域的纳米抗体,随后验证了六种候选物表现出血凝抑制 (HI) 活性。其中,一部分还显示出病毒微中和 (MN) 活性,而所有这些都在 ELISA 测定中显示出结合活性。其中,Nb74对四种中国重组疫苗匹配菌株(Rv1-Rv4)表现出抑制活性,这些菌株是根据相应的灭活疫苗菌株H7-Re1至H7-Re4的HA序列生成的。Rv1–Rv3 的 HI-IC 50 值分别为 0.23、0.57、3.65 和 43.75 μg/mL,MN-IC 50 值分别为 0.02、0.06 和 1.09 μg/mL。尽管 HI 效力因菌株而异,但它还保留了针对不同临床分离株的活性。在小鼠攻击实验中,气管内给予 Nb74 提供了强大的保护,在预防性(2 mg/kg)或治疗性(4 mg/kg)给药时,针对 Rv1 和 Rv2 的存活率分别达到 100% 和 80%。接受治疗的小鼠体重恢复加快,肺部病毒载量减少,肺部病理减轻。机制分析表明,Nb74 通过阻止病毒附着到 宿主 来中和病毒。此外,氢-氘交换质谱 (HDX-MS) 与逃逸突变体分析相结合,将其表位映射到 HA1 亚基上的保守侧斑,与构象表位一致。总体而言,这些结果证明了气管内递送 Nb74 的治疗前景,并为 H7 AIV 疫苗 设计提供了见解。重要性 H7 禽流感 病毒 (AIV),包括低致病性和高致病性菌株,对家禽生产和公共卫生构成持续威胁,而 HA1 亚基的连续抗原 进化 会破坏 疫苗 的有效性。为大流行做好准备,迫切需要针对 H7 AIV 的广泛中和剂,但基于纳米抗体的疗法在很大程度上仍未得到探索。在这项研究中,我们鉴定了 Nb74,一种抑制血凝并中和多种 H7 菌株的纳米抗体。重要的是,Nb74 的气管内递送具有保护功效,支持纳米抗体治疗剂的呼吸给药。机制分析表明,Nb74 通过识别 HA1 亚基侧斑内的保守构象表位来阻止病毒附着。这些发现揭示了 H7 血凝素 (HA) 上的一个保守且功能脆弱的区域,并支持开发基于纳米抗体的 抗病毒药物 和改进的 疫苗 策略,以应对新兴的 H7 AIV 威胁。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
全球范围内已有人类感染 H7 禽流感 病毒的记录,涉及多种亚型和地理区域,但针对 H7 流感病毒的有效治疗方法仍然有限。
设计与对象
该研究通过酵母双杂交筛选鉴定了针对血凝素 HA1 结构域的纳米抗体,并验证了它们在血凝抑制、微中和和 ELISA 测定中的活性。
核心方法
六种纳米抗体候选物经过了血凝抑制活性的验证,其中一个子集显示出微中和活性,并且全部在 ELISA 测定中显示出结合;机理分析使用氢-氘交换质谱和逃逸突变体分析。
主要结果
Nb74 对四种中国重组 疫苗 匹配菌株表现出抑制活性,Rv1–Rv3 的 HI-IC50 值为 0.23、0.57、3.65 和 43.75 μg/mL,MN-IC50 值为 0.02、0.06 和 1.09 μg/mL;它还保留了针对不同临床分离株的活性。
解释与创新
Nb74 通过阻断病毒与 宿主 的附着来中和病毒,靶向 HA1 亚基上的保守侧斑,该亚基代表了治疗干预的功能脆弱区域。
科研与公卫意义
Nb74 的气管内递送在小鼠攻击实验中提供了强大的保护,支持基于纳米抗体的 抗病毒药物 的开发,并改进了针对新出现的 H7 AIV 威胁的 疫苗 策略。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究在体外和小鼠模型中证明了疗效,但需要进一步的临床验证来确认对人类的治疗潜力。

A neutralizing nanobody targeting a conserved lateral patch on HA1 confers protection against multiple H7 avian influenza viruses

Authors: Siqi Xu, Qinying Zhang, Xueer Xie, Mengruo Zhou, Yunxia Chen, Yutong Liu, Chenying Luo, Han Zheng, Saixiang Feng, Ming Liao
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Human infections with H7 avian influenza viruses (AIVs) have been documented globally, involving multiple subtypes and geographic regions. However, effective therapeutics targeting H7 influenza viruses remain limited. Here, a panel of nanobodies targeting the HA1 domain of hemagglutinin (HA) was identified by yeast two-hybrid (Y2H) screening, and six candidates were subsequently validated to exhibit hemagglutination inhibition (HI) activity. Of these, a subset also displayed virus microneutralization (MN) activity, while all showed binding activity in ELISA assays. Among them, Nb74 exhibited inhibitory activity against four Chinese recombinant vaccine-matched strains (Rv1–Rv4), which were generated based on the HA sequences of the corresponding inactivated vaccine strains H7-Re1 to H7-Re4. The HI-IC 50 values were 0.23, 0.57, 3.65, and 43.75 μg/mL, respectively, and the MN-IC 50 values for Rv1–Rv3 were 0.02, 0.06, and 1.09 μg/mL. It also retained activity against diverse clinical isolates although HI potency varied among strains. In mouse challenge experiments, intratracheal administration of Nb74 conferred robust protection, achieving 100% and 80% survival against Rv1 and Rv2, respectively, when administered prophylactically (2 mg/kg) or therapeutically (4 mg/kg). Treated mice showed accelerated body weight recovery, reduced lung viral load, and alleviated pulmonary pathology. Mechanistic analyses indicated that Nb74 neutralizes virus by blocking viral attachment to the host. Furthermore, combined hydrogen-deuterium exchange mass spectrometry (HDX-MS) with escape mutant analysis mapped its epitope to a conserved lateral patch on the HA1 subunit, consistent with a conformational epitope. Overall, these results demonstrate the therapeutic promise of intratracheally delivered Nb74 and provide insights for H7 AIVs vaccine design. IMPORTANCE H7 avian influenza viruses (AIVs), including both low- and highly pathogenic strains, pose a persistent threat to poultry production and public health, while continuous antigenic evolution of the HA1 subunit undermines vaccine effectiveness. Broadly neutralizing agents against H7 AIVs are urgently needed for pandemic preparedness, yet nanobody-based therapeutics remain largely unexplored. In this study, we identified Nb74, a nanobody that inhibits hemagglutination and neutralizes multiple H7 strains. Importantly, intratracheal delivery of Nb74 conferred protective efficacy, supporting respiratory administration of nanobody therapeutics. Mechanistic analyses show that Nb74 blocks viral attachment by recognizing a conserved conformational epitope within a lateral patch of the HA1 subunit. These findings reveal a conserved and functionally vulnerable region on H7 hemagglutinin (HA) and support the development of nanobody-based antivirals and improved vaccine strategies against emerging H7 AIV threats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human infections with H7 avian influenza viruses have been documented globally, involving multiple subtypes and geographic regions, yet effective therapeutics targeting H7 influenza viruses remain limited.
Design and population
The study identified nanobodies targeting the HA1 domain of hemagglutinin through yeast two-hybrid screening and validated their activity in hemagglutination inhibition, microneutralization, and ELISA assays.
Core methods
Six nanobody candidates were validated for hemagglutination inhibition activity, with a subset showing microneutralization activity and all exhibiting binding in ELISA assays; mechanistic analyses used hydrogen-deuterium exchange mass spectrometry and escape mutant analysis.
Main results
Nb74 exhibited inhibitory activity against four Chinese recombinant vaccine-matched strains with HI-IC50 values of 0.23, 0.57, 3.65, and 43.75 μg/mL and MN-IC50 values of 0.02, 0.06, and 1.09 μg/mL for Rv1–Rv3; it also retained activity against diverse clinical isolates.
Interpretation and novelty
Nb74 neutralizes the virus by blocking viral attachment to the host, targeting a conserved lateral patch on the HA1 subunit, which represents a functionally vulnerable region for therapeutic intervention.
Scientific and public-health significance
Intratracheal delivery of Nb74 conferred robust protection in mouse challenge experiments, supporting the development of nanobody-based antivirals and improved vaccine strategies against emerging H7 AIV threats.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study demonstrates efficacy in vitro and in a mouse model, but further clinical validation is needed to confirm therapeutic potential in humans.

高致病性禽流感 A(H5) 的 基因组监测 地理集中度,南亚,2015-2025

Geographic Concentration of Genomic Surveillance for Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza A(H5), South Asia, 2015-2025
作者： Nehal Hasnain, Selim Farhad Shihab, Md. Asadul Islam, Md. Abdur Rahman, Md. Abdul Masum, Shihab, S. F., Rahman, M. A.
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 A(H5) 中哺乳动物适应的早期检测依赖于 基因组监测,但其在高负荷区域的分布特征尚不清楚。我们对 2015 年至 2025 年九个南亚国家报告的 暴发 负担的开放获取 (GenBank/INSDC) H5 基因组覆盖度进行了量化,将分离株与粮农组织 EMPRES-i/WOAH 事件联系起来。在 919 个 H5 分离株中,814 个(89%)来自一个国家(孟加拉国);其他八个菌株贡献了 105 个。印度负担最大(322 个事件),仅产生 42 个分离株(每 100 个分离株 13 个);尼泊尔,78 中的 2;阿富汗,没有 5 个。浓度极端(基尼系数 0.83),并且通过添加限制性 GISAID 记录(1,297 种组合分离株;孟加拉国 89%)或通过对家禽或人群进行标准化而保持不变。由于报告的 暴发 跟踪报告工作,因此这些覆盖率是方向性的,而不是比率。这种单一国家依赖性最深,负担也最重,是一个区域预警脆弱性。

问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 A(H5) 中哺乳动物适应的早期检测依赖于 基因组监测,但其在高负荷区域的分布特征尚不清楚。
设计与对象
我们对 2015 年至 2025 年九个南亚国家报告的 暴发 负担的开放获取 (GenBank/INSDC) H5 基因组覆盖度进行了量化,将分离株与粮农组织 EMPRES-i/WOAH 事件联系起来。
核心方法
在 919 个 H5 分离株中,814 个(89%)来自一个国家(孟加拉国);其他八人贡献了105。
主要结果
印度负担最大(322 个事件),仅产生 42 个分离株(每 100 个分离株 13 个);尼泊尔,78 中的 2;阿富汗,5个都没有。
解释与创新
浓度是极端的(基尼系数 0.83),并且通过添加限制性 GISAID 记录(1,297 种组合分离株;孟加拉国 89%)或通过对家禽或人群进行标准化而保持不变。

由于报告的 暴发 跟踪报告工作,因此这些覆盖率是方向性的,而不是比率。
局限与证据强度
这种单一国家依赖性最深,负担也最重,是一个区域预警脆弱性。

Geographic Concentration of Genomic Surveillance for Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza A(H5), South Asia, 2015-2025

Authors: Nehal Hasnain, Selim Farhad Shihab, Md. Asadul Islam, Md. Abdur Rahman, Md. Abdul Masum, Shihab, S. F., Rahman, M. A.
Original abstract (English)
Early detection of mammalian adaptation in highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5) depends on genomic surveillance, yet its distribution across high-burden regions is poorly characterized. We quantified open-access (GenBank/INSDC) H5 genomic coverage relative to reported outbreak burden across nine South Asian countries during 2015-2025, linking isolates to FAO EMPRES-i/WOAH events. Of 919 H5 isolates, 814 (89%) came from one country (Bangladesh); the other eight contributed 105. India, with the largest burden (322 events), yielded only 42 isolates (13 per 100); Nepal, 2 of 78; Afghanistan, none of 5. Concentration was extreme (Gini 0.83) and unchanged by adding restricted GISAID records (1,297 combined isolates; Bangladesh 89%) or by normalizing to poultry or human population. Because reported outbreaks track reporting effort, these coverage ratios are directional, not rates. This single-country dependency, deepest where burden is highest, is a regional early-warning vulnerability.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Early detection of mammalian adaptation in highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5) depends on genomic surveillance, yet its distribution across high-burden regions is poorly characterized.
Design and population
We quantified open-access (GenBank/INSDC) H5 genomic coverage relative to reported outbreak burden across nine South Asian countries during 2015-2025, linking isolates to FAO EMPRES-i/WOAH events.
Core methods
Of 919 H5 isolates, 814 (89%) came from one country (Bangladesh); the other eight contributed 105.
Main results
India, with the largest burden (322 events), yielded only 42 isolates (13 per 100); Nepal, 2 of 78; Afghanistan, none of 5.
Interpretation and novelty
Concentration was extreme (Gini 0.83) and unchanged by adding restricted GISAID records (1,297 combined isolates; Bangladesh 89%) or by normalizing to poultry or human population.
Scientific and public-health significance
Because reported outbreaks track reporting effort, these coverage ratios are directional, not rates.
Limitations and evidence strength
This single-country dependency, deepest where burden is highest, is a regional early-warning vulnerability.

专家警告说,秘鲁唯一的企鹅物种面临即将到来的厄尔尼诺现象和禽流感的威胁

Peru’s only penguin species at risk from upcoming El Niño and bird flu, experts warn
作者： Dan Collyns
摘要中文翻译秘鲁圣胡安德马科纳——首先是 2022 年的 H5N1 禽流感,然后是 2023 年的厄尔尼诺现象,扰乱了沿海水域和鱼类供应。对于秘鲁唯一在该国繁殖的企鹅物种——洪堡企鹅来说,负担太重:2022年至2025年间,其数量减少了三分之二。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了 H5N1 禽流感 和厄尔尼诺现象对秘鲁洪堡企鹅种群的影响,强调了洪堡企鹅种群数量的显着下降是一个紧迫的保护问题。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注秘鲁洪堡企鹅,这是该国唯一繁殖的企鹅物种,追踪了 2022 年至 2025 年间的种群数据。
核心方法
该研究分析了种群趋势和环境破坏,特别研究了 H5N1 禽流感 和厄尔尼诺现象对企鹅栖息地和食物供应的影响。
主要结果
2022 年至 2025 年间,由于 H5N1 禽流感 和厄尔尼诺现象的综合影响,秘鲁洪堡企鹅数量减少了三分之二。
解释与创新
作者将这种下降解释为双重压力源的直接后果,强调该物种对同时存在的环境和疾病相关威胁的脆弱性前所未有。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要加强 监测 和气候相关对濒危物种的影响,为保护策略和人畜共患疾病监测提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于观察性人群数据,没有详细的机制研究,由于缺乏对照分析,导致可信度中等。

Peru’s only penguin species at risk from upcoming El Niño and bird flu, experts warn

Authors: Dan Collyns
Original abstract (English)
SAN JUAN DE MARCONA, Peru — First, there was the H5N1 avian influenza of 2022, then El Niño arrived in 2023, disrupting coastal waters and fish availability. For Peru’s Humboldt penguins, the only species of penguins that breeds in the country, the burden was too heavy: Its population dropped by two-thirds between 2022 and 2025.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the impact of H5N1 avian influenza and El Niño on Peru's Humboldt penguin population, highlighting a significant decline as a pressing conservation concern.
Design and population
The research focuses on Peru's Humboldt penguins, the only penguin species breeding in the country, with population data tracked between 2022 and 2025.
Core methods
The study analyzes population trends and environmental disruptions, specifically examining the effects of H5N1 avian influenza and El Niño on the penguins' habitat and food availability.
Main results
Peru's Humboldt penguin population decreased by two-thirds between 2022 and 2025, attributed to the combined impacts of H5N1 avian influenza and El Niño.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the decline as a direct consequence of dual stressors, emphasizing the unprecedented vulnerability of this species to concurrent environmental and disease-related threats.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for enhanced surveillance of avian influenza and climate-related impacts on endangered species, informing conservation strategies and zoonotic disease monitoring.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to observational population data without detailed mechanistic studies, resulting in moderate confidence due to the lack of controlled analysis.

H3N2 犬流感病毒细胞和卵优先复制的遗传决定因素。

Genetic determinants of cell- and egg-preferred replication in H3N2 canine influenza virus.
作者： Rang Wang, Yaohui Zhu, Min Zhu, Xiaolong Li, Huabo Zhou, Linying Peng, Shiqi Li, Haodong Zhang, Shanshan Dong, Yifeng Qin
摘要中文翻译H3N2 犬流感病毒 (CIV) 起源于 禽流感 病毒,已在狗中传播了二十多年,但对其 宿主 适应机制和细胞趋向性仍知之甚少。在这项研究中,我们从犬呼吸道疾病 暴发 中分离出两种表型不同的 H3N2 CIV 毒株(CX3 和 CX12)。尽管两者都是使用 MDCK 细胞分离的,但 CX3 在 MDCK 细胞中表现出更高的复制效率和噬斑形成能力,而 CX12 在鸡胚胎中复制效率更高。利用反向遗传学,我们确定了 PB1 基因是病毒聚合酶活性的关键增强子,而 HA 基因是病毒稳定性的关键贡献者,这两者共同介导细胞偏好的复制。这些发现为 H3N2 CIV 宿主 适应提供了机制见解,并为未来的 疫苗 开发奠定了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解H3N2犬流感病毒的宿主适应机制和细胞向性,该病毒起源于禽流感病毒,已在狗中传播了二十多年,但在这些方面仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,从犬呼吸道疾病 暴发 中分离出两种表型不同的 H3N2 CIV 毒株(CX3 和 CX12)。
核心方法
研究人员利用反向遗传学,确定了 PB1 基因是病毒聚合酶活性的关键增强子,而 HA 基因是病毒稳定性的关键贡献者,以研究它们在病毒复制中的作用。
主要结果
CX3 在 MDCK 细胞中表现出更高的复制效率和噬斑形成能力,而 CX12 在鸡胚中复制效率更高,其中 PB1 和 HA 基因共同介导细胞偏好的复制。
解释与创新
这些发现为 H3N2 CIV 宿主 适应提供了机制见解,并为未来的 疫苗 开发奠定了基础。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解病毒的趋向性和适应性,这可能为犬流感病毒的 监测 策略和 疫苗 设计提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于有限菌株的体外实验,由于调查范围狭窄,证据强度中等。

Genetic determinants of cell- and egg-preferred replication in H3N2 canine influenza virus.

Authors: Rang Wang, Yaohui Zhu, Min Zhu, Xiaolong Li, Huabo Zhou, Linying Peng, Shiqi Li, Haodong Zhang, Shanshan Dong, Yifeng Qin
Original abstract (English)
The H3N2 canine influenza virus (CIV), which originated from avian influenza viruses and has circulated in dogs for over two decades, remains poorly understood in terms of its host adaptation mechanisms and cellular tropism. In this study, we isolated two phenotypically distinct H3N2 CIV strains (CX3 and CX12) from a canine respiratory disease outbreak. Although both were isolated using MDCK cells, CX3 showed higher replication efficiency and plaque-forming ability in MDCK cells, whereas CX12 replicated more efficiently in chicken embryos. Using reverse genetics, we identified the PB1 gene as a critical enhancer of viral polymerase activity and the HA gene as a key contributor to viral stability, both of which collectively mediate cell-preferred replication. These findings provide mechanistic insights into H3N2 CIV host adaptation and offer a foundation for future vaccine development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the host adaptation mechanisms and cellular tropism of H3N2 canine influenza virus, which originated from avian influenza viruses and has circulated in dogs for over two decades but remains poorly understood in these aspects.
Design and population
In this study, two phenotypically distinct H3N2 CIV strains (CX3 and CX12) were isolated from a canine respiratory disease outbreak.
Core methods
Using reverse genetics, the researchers identified the PB1 gene as a critical enhancer of viral polymerase activity and the HA gene as a key contributor to viral stability to investigate their roles in viral replication.
Main results
CX3 showed higher replication efficiency and plaque-forming ability in MDCK cells, whereas CX12 replicated more efficiently in chicken embryos, with the PB1 and HA genes collectively mediating cell-preferred replication.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide mechanistic insights into H3N2 CIV host adaptation and offer a foundation for future vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results contribute to understanding viral tropism and adaptation, which may inform surveillance strategies and vaccine design for canine influenza viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on in vitro experiments with limited strains, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the narrow scope of the investigation.

致命的人类 H5N5 流感病毒分离株表现出低大流行风险特征

A lethal human H5N5 influenza virus isolate exhibits low pandemic risk traits
作者： Seema S. Lakdawala, Michelle N. Vu, Grace E Quirk, Alexis E Smathers, Kaitlyn Bushfield-Thomason, Alaina L Dorazio, G M Humber, John Sembrat, Anita K. McElroy, Valerie M. Le Sage
摘要中文翻译2025年秋天,发生了高致病性禽流感(HPAI)病毒H5N5的致命感染。为了确定这种新出现的病毒对人类的风险,我们根据既定的分类进行了全面分析。血清学分析显示,所有出生年份的人类都没有检测到针对这种 H5N5 分离株的中和抗体。进一步的表征表明,缺乏与人类流行病学上成功的流感病毒相关的表型特征,包括人类呼吸道细胞中复制的减少和类似禽类的失活pH值。此外,对雪貂 H5N5 的评估显示缺乏直接接触 传播 且疾病严重程度中等。先前对 2009 年 H1N1 大流行毒株具有免疫力的雪貂感染 H5N5 后,临床症状减少,病毒脱落减少。我们的数据综合表明,当前 H5N5 HPAI 谱系构成大流行的风险较低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2025 年秋季,发生了 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 病毒 H5N5 的致命感染,促使需要评估其对人类的大流行风险。
设计与对象
为了确定这种新出现的病毒对人类的风险,研究人员根据既定的分类进行了全面分析。
核心方法
该研究涉及血清学分析,以检测所有出生年份的人类中和抗体,并表征病毒的表型特征,包括在人类呼吸道细胞中的复制和失活的 pH 值。
主要结果
血清学分析显示,所有出生年份的人类都没有检测到针对这种 H5N5 分离株的中和抗体,进一步的表征表明,人类气道细胞的复制减少,且失活的 pH 值与鸟类相似。
解释与创新
作者解释说,缺乏可检测到的中和抗体和与成功的人类流感病毒相关的表型特征,以及雪貂中有限的 传播,表明当前的 H5N5 HPAI 谱系具有较低的大流行风险。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 监测 和新兴 禽流感 病毒的风险评估提供了信息,表明 H5N5 目前对人类构成的威胁有限,但值得继续监测。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于体外和动物模型数据,尽管综合分析提供了可靠的初步风险评估,但可能无法完全预测人类 传播 动态。

A lethal human H5N5 influenza virus isolate exhibits low pandemic risk traits

Authors: Seema S. Lakdawala, Michelle N. Vu, Grace E Quirk, Alexis E Smathers, Kaitlyn Bushfield-Thomason, Alaina L Dorazio, G M Humber, John Sembrat, Anita K. McElroy, Valerie M. Le Sage
Original abstract (English)
In fall of 2025, a fatal infection of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) virus H5N5 occurred. To define the risk of this emerging virus to humans, we performed a comprehensive analysis based on our established triage. Serological analysis revealed that humans across all birth years had no detectable neutralizing antibodies to this H5N5 isolate. Further characterization revealed a lack of phenotypic signatures associated with epidemiologically successful influenza viruses in humans, including reduced replication in human airway cells and an avian-like pH of inactivation. Additionally, assessment of H5N5 in ferrets revealed a lack of direct contact transmission and moderate disease severity. H5N5 infection in ferrets with prior immunity against the 2009 H1N1 pandemic strain resulted in fewer clinical signs and reduced viral shedding. Together our data suggest that the current H5N5 HPAI lineage poses a low pandemic risk.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In fall of 2025, a fatal infection of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) virus H5N5 occurred, prompting a need to assess its pandemic risk to humans.
Design and population
To define the risk of this emerging virus to humans, the researchers performed a comprehensive analysis based on their established triage.
Core methods
The study involved serological analysis to detect neutralizing antibodies in humans across all birth years and characterization of the virus's phenotypic signatures, including replication in human airway cells and pH of inactivation.
Main results
Serological analysis revealed that humans across all birth years had no detectable neutralizing antibodies to this H5N5 isolate, and further characterization showed reduced replication in human airway cells and an avian-like pH of inactivation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the lack of detectable neutralizing antibodies and phenotypic signatures associated with successful human influenza viruses, along with limited transmission in ferrets, indicates the current H5N5 HPAI lineage poses a low pandemic risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform surveillance and risk assessment for emerging avian influenza viruses, suggesting that H5N5 currently poses limited threat to human populations but warrants continued monitoring.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on in vitro and animal model data, which may not fully predict human transmission dynamics, though the comprehensive analysis provides robust preliminary risk assessment.

Journal: American Journal of Public Health  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-01  |  DOI: 10.2105/ajph.2026.308445  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 116, 8, 1220-1228
C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

美国流感中人与动物界面的一个健康盲点 监测。

The One Health Blind Spot at the Human‒Animal Interface in US Influenza Surveillance.
作者： Cole King, A Paige Adams
摘要中文翻译新型甲型流感病毒在 21 世纪造成了数千人的生命和数十亿美元的经济损失,过去 4 次大流行甲型流感病毒中有 2 次可能起源于北美大陆的动物种群。尽管如此,美国的人类和兽用流感 监测 系统连接较差,并且未达到国会对数据共享和互操作性的要求。这使美国处于一种被动的姿态,重点是遏制已经发生的暴发,而不是积极主动的暴发和预防。COVID-19 大流行推动了对 监测 基础设施和基因组技术的重大投资,以及新的国家统一健康框架,为实现流感 监测 现代化提供了独特的机会,同时更加强调人类和动物健康的相互联系。然而,随着大流行时代资金的退出,增强型 监测 的资金将大幅减少。对新型 监测 技术的持续投资对于解决最近的 H5N1 流感 暴发 至关重要,对于防止未来的经济损失以及人类和动物生命的损失也至关重要。(《美国公共卫生杂志》。2026;116(8):1220-1228。https://doi.org/10.2105/AJPH.2026.308445)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了美国人类和兽用流感 监测 系统相互关联的关键差距,该系统未能满足国会数据共享的要求,导致该国在预防流感 暴发 方面处于被动而非主动的状态。
设计与对象
该分析重点关注美国流感 监测 基础设施、基因组技术和国家统一健康框架,强调在人与动物界面上增强 监测 的必要性。
核心方法
该评估基于对当前 监测 系统、基因组技术和政策框架的审查,重点关注它们在检测新型流感病毒方面的整合和有效性。
主要结果
尽管 COVID-19 大流行和国家统一健康框架的建立刺激了巨额投资,但美国 监测 系统连接较差,导致采取被动反应而非主动预防。
解释与创新
作者将缺乏集成的 监测 解释为一个主要盲点,强调了通过 One Health 方法实现系统现代化的机会,尽管增强型 监测 的资金将大幅减少。
科研与公卫意义
对新型 监测 技术的持续投资对于解决最近的 H5N1 暴发 问题并防止未来的经济和人员生命损失至关重要,这凸显了持续资金和数据共享的必要性。
局限与证据强度
证据基于抽象层面的分析,可能缺乏详细的方法论严谨性,但高层政策和 监测 见解提供了对当前差距和机遇的适度评估。

The One Health Blind Spot at the Human‒Animal Interface in US Influenza Surveillance.

Authors: Cole King, A Paige Adams
Original abstract (English)
Novel influenza A viruses have cost thousands of lives and billions of dollars of economic losses in the 21st century, and 2 of the past 4 pandemic influenza A viruses likely originated in animal populations on the North American continent. Despite this, the human and veterinary influenza surveillance systems in the United States are poorly connected and have fallen short of congressional mandates for data sharing and interoperability. This places the United States in a reactive posture that is focused on containment of outbreaks that have already occurred rather than proactive surveillance and prevention. The significant investments in surveillance infrastructure and genomic technologies spurred by the COVID-19 pandemic, along with the new National One Health Framework, present a unique opportunity to modernize influenza surveillance with a greater emphasis on the interconnectedness of human and animal health. However, funding for enhanced surveillance is set to decrease significantly as pandemic-era funding sunsets. Continuing investments into novel surveillance technologies have been critical in addressing the recent H5N1 influenza outbreaks and will be critical to prevent future economic losses and losses of both human and animal life. ( Am J Public Health . 2026;116(8):1220-1228. https://doi.org/10.2105/AJPH.2026.308445).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the critical gap in interconnected human and veterinary influenza surveillance systems in the United States, which have failed to meet congressional mandates for data sharing, leaving the country reactive rather than proactive in preventing influenza outbreaks.
Design and population
The analysis focuses on the US influenza surveillance infrastructure, genomic technologies, and the National One Health Framework, emphasizing the need for enhanced surveillance at the human-animal interface.
Core methods
The evaluation is based on a review of current surveillance systems, genomic technologies, and policy frameworks, with a focus on their integration and effectiveness in detecting novel influenza viruses.
Main results
The US surveillance systems are poorly connected, leading to a reactive posture rather than proactive prevention, despite significant investments spurred by the COVID-19 pandemic and the establishment of the National One Health Framework.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the lack of integrated surveillance as a major blind spot, highlighting the opportunity to modernize systems with a One Health approach, though funding for enhanced surveillance is set to decrease significantly.
Scientific and public-health significance
Continued investments in novel surveillance technologies are critical for addressing recent H5N1 outbreaks and preventing future economic and human life losses, underscoring the need for sustained funding and data sharing.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on abstract-level analysis, which may lack detailed methodological rigor, but the high-level policy and surveillance insights provide a moderate assessment of the current gaps and opportunities.

进化 并不总是分叉:ATLAZ 和网状病毒学的几何分辨率

Evolution is Not Always Bifurcating: ATLAZ and the Geometric Resolution of Reticulate Virology
作者： Md Arshad
摘要中文翻译摘要 对严格分叉的系统发育树的依赖从根本上扭曲了网状病毒种群的进化历史。传统的最大似然算法要求垂直下降,将人工进化枝强加于重组 基因组 上,并模糊了分段流行病转变的起源。此外,在评估重叠的开放阅读框时,线性选择指标(例如 dN/dS)在数学上会失败。在这里,ATLAZ(Zig 中的对齐、拓扑和谱系分析)被视为一种内存确定性几何引擎,它明确绕过系统发育树来跟踪从树状到网状 进化 的主要转变。通过计算 Vietoris-Rips 单纯复合体并直接从空间距离矩阵中提取 H1 持久性,ATLAZ 明确分离了乙型肝炎和 禽流感 中的水平重组。相反,拓扑环的缺失严格证明了埃博拉病毒、寨卡病毒和马尔堡病毒的克隆血统。利用跨零拷贝 C-ABI 边界的原生伽罗瓦域缩减,ATLAZ 可以处理大量 队列研究(超过 6,000 个序列),内存严格受稀疏过滤截断限制,打破了先前拓扑数据分析框架的灾难性计算瓶颈。此外,顺序列消融计算出一种新颖的拓扑选择分数(TSS),以几何方式映射绝对结构刚性并在几秒钟内确定直接作用的 抗病毒药物 的最佳目标。ATLAZ 建立了一个新的绝对的、数学驱动的标准,用于跟踪计算病毒学的重大转变。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了网状病毒种群严格分叉的系统发育树造成的进化历史的根本扭曲,旨在解决分段大流行转变的起源。
设计与对象
该研究引入了ATLAZ,一种记忆确定性几何引擎,并评估了其在乙型肝炎、禽流感、埃博拉、寨卡和马尔堡病毒等病毒序列上的性能。
核心方法
ATLAZ 计算 Vietoris-Rips 单纯复形,并从空间距离矩阵中提取 H1 持久性以跟踪进化转变,利用伽罗瓦域约简处理具有有限内存的 6,000 多个序列。
主要结果
ATLAZ 明确分离了乙型肝炎和 禽流感 中的水平重组,同时证明了埃博拉病毒、寨卡病毒和马尔堡病毒的克隆血统,并计算了 抗病毒药物 目标识别的拓扑选择分数。
解释与创新
作者将 ATLAZ 解释为建立一个新的数学驱动标准,用于跟踪计算病毒学的主要转变,绕过传统的系统发育树来解析网状 进化。
科研与公卫意义
ATLAZ 能够实现结构刚性的几何映射,以确定直接作用的 抗病毒药物 的最佳靶点,针对 禽流感 等快速进化的病毒推进 监测 和 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
尽管计算效率和数学严谨性表明可信度中等,但证据仅限于抽象的发现,没有详细的验证或与现有方法的比较分析。

Evolution is Not Always Bifurcating: ATLAZ and the Geometric Resolution of Reticulate Virology

Authors: Md Arshad
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The reliance on strictly bifurcating phylogenetic trees fundamentally distorts the evolutionary history of reticulate viral populations. Traditional maximum-likelihood algorithms mandate vertical descent, imposing artificial clades upon recombinant genomes and obscuring the origins of segmented pandemic shifts. Furthermore, linear selection metrics (e.g., dN/dS) fail mathematically when evaluating overlapping open reading frames. Here, ATLAZ (Alignment, Topology, and Lineage Analysis in Zig) is presented as a memory-deterministic geometric engine that explicitly bypasses the phylogenetic tree to track the major transition from tree-like to reticulate evolution. By computing Vietoris-Rips simplicial complexes and extracting H1 persistence directly from spatial distance matrices, ATLAZ definitively isolates horizontal recombination in Hepatitis B and Avian Influenza. Conversely, the absence of topological loops strictly proves the clonal descent of Ebola, Zika, and Marburg viruses. Utilizing a native Galois Field reduction across a zero-copy C-ABI boundary, ATLAZ processes massive cohorts (over 6,000 sequences) with memory strictly bounded by the sparse filtration cutoff, shattering the catastrophic computational bottlenecks of previous Topological Data Analysis frameworks. Furthermore, sequential column ablation calculates a novel Topological Selection Score (TSS), geometrically mapping absolute structural rigidity and identifying optimal targets for direct-acting antivirals in seconds. ATLAZ establishes a new absolute, math-driven standard for tracking major transitions in computational virology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the fundamental distortion in evolutionary history caused by strictly bifurcating phylogenetic trees for reticulate viral populations, aiming to resolve the origins of segmented pandemic shifts.
Design and population
The research introduces ATLAZ, a memory-deterministic geometric engine, and evaluates its performance on viral sequences including Hepatitis B, Avian Influenza, Ebola, Zika, and Marburg viruses.
Core methods
ATLAZ computes Vietoris-Rips simplicial complexes and extracts H1 persistence from spatial distance matrices to track evolutionary transitions, utilizing Galois Field reduction to process over 6,000 sequences with bounded memory.
Main results
ATLAZ definitively isolates horizontal recombination in Hepatitis B and Avian Influenza, while proving clonal descent for Ebola, Zika, and Marburg viruses, and calculates a Topological Selection Score for antiviral target identification.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret ATLAZ as establishing a new math-driven standard for tracking major transitions in computational virology, bypassing traditional phylogenetic trees to resolve reticulate evolution.
Scientific and public-health significance
ATLAZ enables geometric mapping of structural rigidity to identify optimal targets for direct-acting antivirals, advancing surveillance and treatment strategies for rapidly evolving viruses like Avian Influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to abstract-only findings without detailed validation or comparative analysis against existing methods, though the computational efficiency and mathematical rigor suggest moderate confidence.

通过常规血凝素免疫原在恒河猴中诱导干定向抗体

Elicitation of stem-directed antibodies in rhesus macaques by a conventional hemagglutinin immunogen
作者： Shuqin Gu, Joel Finney, Kan Luo, Sarah Valencia, Dieter Mielke, Tarra Von Holle, Marshall DJ, Robert Parks, Laura L. Sutherland, Richard M. Scearce
摘要中文翻译由于它们可以结合多种流感病毒株,因此针对血凝素 (HA) 茎的抗体一直是 疫苗 开发的有吸引力的目标。许多针对 HA 茎的单克隆抗体 (mAb) 已从人类体内分离出来,这些 mAb 在动物模型中发挥了广泛的保护作用。我们在这里描述了从用“普通”H1 HA 三聚体免疫的恒河猴中分离出的 HA 干定向单克隆抗体。所有免疫的恒河猴均产生高血清滴度,对多种 H1N1 和 H5N1 病毒具有广泛的反应性,7 种分离的 mAb 强烈阻断经典干抗体 CR6261 与 H1 的结合。MAb DH726.1 强有力地保护小鼠免受 H1N1 和 H5N1 病毒的致命攻击,冷冻电镜显示结合足迹与一些人类 mAb 的结合足迹重叠。这些发现表明,疫苗接种 与标准三聚体 HA 免疫原可能足以引发干抗体,其滴度足以预防人畜共患 H5N1 流感。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨传统的血凝素(HA)免疫原是否可以在恒河猴中引发干定向抗体,这对于广泛预防流感病毒至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用普通 H1 HA 三聚体对恒河猴进行免疫,并从其血清中分离单克隆抗体 (mAb)。所有经过免疫的猕猴都产生了高血清滴度,对多种 H1N1 和 H5N1 病毒具有广泛的反应性。
核心方法
研究人员用 H1 HA 三聚体对恒河猴进行免疫,并从其血清中分离出单克隆抗体。然后,他们评估了抗体对各种 H1N1 和 H5N1 病毒的反应性,并评估了它们在小鼠中的中和活性。
主要结果
所有免疫的恒河猴均产生高血清滴度,对多种 H1N1 和 H5N1 病毒具有广泛的反应性,7 种分离的 mAb 强烈阻断经典干抗体 CR6261 与 H1 的结合。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,疫苗接种 与标准三聚体 HA 免疫原可能足以引发干抗体,其滴度足以预防人畜共患 H5N1 流感。
科研与公卫意义
结果提供了对传统 HA 免疫原产生保护性干抗体的潜力的见解,这可以为 疫苗 的 禽流感 开发策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于动物模型,不包括人体试验,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。由于依赖体内模型,证据强度中等。

Elicitation of stem-directed antibodies in rhesus macaques by a conventional hemagglutinin immunogen

Authors: Shuqin Gu, Joel Finney, Kan Luo, Sarah Valencia, Dieter Mielke, Tarra Von Holle, Marshall DJ, Robert Parks, Laura L. Sutherland, Richard M. Scearce
Original abstract (English)
Because they can bind many strains of influenza, antibodies targeting the hemagglutinin (HA) stem have been attractive targets for vaccine development. Many monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) directed at the HA stem have been isolated from humans, and these mAbs have mediated broad protection in animal models. We describe here HA stem-directed mAbs isolated from rhesus macaques immunized with an "ordinary" H1 HA trimer. All immunized rhesus macaques developed high serum titers with broad reactivity to diverse H1N1 and H5N1 viruses, and 7 isolated mAbs strongly blocked canonical stem antibody CR6261 binding to H1. MAb DH726.1 robustly protected mice from lethal challenge with H1N1 and H5N1 viruses, and cryo-EM showed the binding footprint overlapped that of some human mAbs. These findings suggest that vaccination with the standard, trimeric HA immunogens may be sufficient to elicit stem antibodies at titers adequate to protect against zoonotic H5N1 influenza.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore whether conventional hemagglutinin (HA) immunogens could elicit stem-directed antibodies in rhesus macaques, which are critical for broad protection against influenza viruses.
Design and population
The study involved immunizing rhesus macaques with an ordinary H1 HA trimer and isolating monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) from their sera. All immunized macaques developed high serum titers with broad reactivity to diverse H1N1 and H5N1 viruses.
Core methods
The researchers immunized rhesus macaques with an H1 HA trimer and isolated monoclonal antibodies from their sera. They then assessed the antibodies' reactivity against various H1N1 and H5N1 viruses and evaluated their neutralizing activity in mice.
Main results
All immunized rhesus macaques developed high serum titers with broad reactivity to diverse H1N1 and H5N1 viruses, and 7 isolated mAbs strongly blocked canonical stem antibody CR6261 binding to H1.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that vaccination with standard, trimeric HA immunogens may be sufficient to elicit stem antibodies at titers adequate to protect against zoonotic H5N1 influenza.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the potential of conventional HA immunogens to generate protective stem antibodies, which could inform vaccine development strategies for avian influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to animal models and does not include human trials, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on in vivo models.

综合计算机和体外方法揭示了针对甲型流感病毒 RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶的新型香豆素杂合体。

Integrated in silico and in vitro approaches reveal novel coumarin hybrids targeting influenza A virus RNA-dependent RNA polymerase.
作者： Maria Laura Goussain Darido, Mariana Luíza da Silva, Ana Heloisa Fonseca Ayala, Ana Carolina Ramos Gimenes, Priscila Goes Camargo, Nicole Oberberg, Franziska Rodner, Carla Cristina Perez, Camilo Henrique da Silva Lima, Danielle Bruna Leal de Oliveira
摘要中文翻译摘要:甲型流感病毒(IAV)由于其高 突变 发病率以及对现有 抗病毒药物 药物的耐药性不断增加,仍然是一个重大的公共卫生威胁。病毒 RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶 (RdRp) 对于病毒复制至关重要,是新疗法的一个有希望的靶标。我们使用分子对接针对 IAV RdRp 复合物的两个结构域筛选了包含 103 种化合物的内部化学库。根据计算结果,我们选择了 11 种香豆素-酚酸杂化物和一种噻唑烷酮化合物,用于针对 H1N1 和 H5N1 的体外评估。LMed 93 和 LMed 99 表现出有效的 抗病毒药物 活性,针对 H1N1 的 EC 50 值分别为 5.66 和 1.21 μM,并且具有高选择性指数。机制研究表明,LMed 99 在感染的早期到晚期阶段发挥作用,而 LMed 93 则影响早期和中期阶段。两种化合物均显着抑制病毒 RNA 合成,LMed 99 在感染后 1 小时和 3 小时 (hpi) 时实现了 82% 的抑制,在 6 hpi 时实现了 80% 的抑制,LMed 93 实现了 82% (1 hpi)、78% (3 hpi) 和 60% (6 hpi)。LMed 99 以时间依赖性方式减少病毒蛋白和 mRNA,表明对病毒转录的干扰。小型复制子测定显示,70 和 35 μM 的 LMed 93 使聚合酶活性降低 53.7% 和 38.7%,而 9 和 4.5 μM 的 LMed 99 分别使表达增加 108.97% 和 61.37%。结构-活性关系分析强调,儿茶酚基团和卤化增强了 抗病毒药物 的效力。这些研究结果表明,LMed 93 和 LMed 99 是有前途的基于香豆素的支架,可用于开发新型抗甲型流感药物,也为对抗其他 RNA 病毒提供了潜在的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于 突变 的高发病率以及对现有 抗病毒药物 药物的耐药性不断增加,甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生威胁。该研究旨在鉴定针对 IAV RNA 依赖性 RNA 聚合酶 (RdRp) 的新型化合物来应对这一挑战。
设计与对象
该研究采用计算机和体外方法来筛选针对 IAV RdRp 复合物的化合物。它重点关注 禽流感 A 病毒,特别是 H5N1,并评估了所选化合物的 抗病毒药物 体外活性。
核心方法
研究人员利用分子对接筛选了针对 IAV RdRp 复合物两个结构域的 103 种化合物。根据计算结果,他们选择了 11 种香豆素-酚酸杂化物和一种噻唑烷酮化合物,用于针对 H1N1 和 H5N1 的体外评估。
主要结果
LMed 93 和 LMed 99 表现出有效的 抗病毒药物 活性,针对 H1N1 的 EC 50 值分别为 5.66 和 1.21 μM,并且具有高选择性指数。两种化合物均显着抑制病毒 RNA 合成,LMed 99 在感染后 1 小时和 3 小时 (hpi) 时实现了 82% 的抑制,在 6 hpi 时实现了 80% 的抑制,LMed 93 实现了 82% (1 hpi)、78% (3 hpi) 和 60% (6 hpi)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,LMed 93 和 LMed 99 是有前途的基于香豆素的支架,可用于开发新型抗甲型流感药物,为对抗其他 RNA 病毒提供潜在策略。
科研与公卫意义
结果为针对 IAV RdRp 的新 抗病毒药物 药物的开发提供了见解,这可以增强 禽流感 暴发 的 监测 和 治疗 选项。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外研究结果和计算筛选,可能无法完全反映体内疗效。证据是中等的,因为它不包括临床试验或长期影响。

Integrated in silico and in vitro approaches reveal novel coumarin hybrids targeting influenza A virus RNA-dependent RNA polymerase.

Authors: Maria Laura Goussain Darido, Mariana Luíza da Silva, Ana Heloisa Fonseca Ayala, Ana Carolina Ramos Gimenes, Priscila Goes Camargo, Nicole Oberberg, Franziska Rodner, Carla Cristina Perez, Camilo Henrique da Silva Lima, Danielle Bruna Leal de Oliveira
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT: Influenza A virus (IAV) remains a significant public health threat due to its high mutation rate and increasing resistance to existing antiviral drugs. The viral RNA-dependent RNA polymerase (RdRp) is essential for viral replication and represents a promising target for new therapeutics. We screened our in-house chemical library of 103 compounds against two domains of the IAV RdRp complex using molecular docking. Based on computational findings, we selected eleven coumarin-phenolic acid hybrids and one thiazolidinone compounds for in vitro evaluation against H1N1 and H5N1. LMed 93 and LMed 99 exhibited potent antiviral activity, with EC 50 values of 5.66 and 1.21 μM against H1N1 respectively, and high selectivity indices. Mechanistic studies showed that LMed 99 acts throughout early to late stages of infection, while LMed 93 impacts early and intermediate stages. Both compounds significantly inhibited viral RNA synthesis, with LMed 99 achieving 82% inhibition at 1- and 3-hours post-infection (hpi) and 80% at 6 hpi, and LMed 93 achieving 82% (1 hpi), 78% (3 hpi), and 60% (6 hpi). LMed 99 reduced viral proteins and mRNA in a time-dependent manner, indicating interference with viral transcription. A mini-replicon assay showed that LMed 93, at 70 and 35 μM, reduces polymerase activity by 53.7% and 38.7%, while LMed 99 at 9 and 4.5 μM increased expression by 108.97% and 61.37%, respectively. Structure–activity relationship analysis highlighted that catechol groups and halogenation enhance antiviral potency. These findings suggest that LMed 93 and LMed 99 are promising coumarin-based scaffolds for developing new anti-Influenza A agents, also offering potential strategies for combating other RNA viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A virus (IAV) remains a significant public health threat due to its high mutation rate and increasing resistance to existing antiviral drugs. The study aimed to identify novel compounds targeting IAV RNA-dependent RNA polymerase (RdRp) to address this challenge.
Design and population
The study employed an in silico and in vitro approach to screen compounds against the IAV RdRp complex. It focused on avian influenza A viruses, specifically H5N1, and evaluated the antiviral activity of selected compounds in vitro.
Core methods
The researchers used molecular docking to screen 103 compounds against two domains of the IAV RdRp complex. Based on computational findings, they selected eleven coumarin-phenolic acid hybrids and one thiazolidinone compound for in vitro evaluation against H1N1 and H5N1.
Main results
LMed 93 and LMed 99 exhibited potent antiviral activity, with EC 50 values of 5.66 and 1.21 μM against H1N1, respectively, and high selectivity indices. Both compounds significantly inhibited viral RNA synthesis, with LMed 99 achieving 82% inhibition at 1- and 3-hours post-infection (hpi) and 80% at 6 hpi, and LMed 93 achieving 82% (1 hpi), 78% (3 hpi), and 60% (6 hpi).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that LMed 93 and LMed 99 are promising coumarin-based scaffolds for developing new anti-Influenza A agents, offering potential strategies for combating other RNA viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the development of new antiviral agents targeting IAV RdRp, which could enhance surveillance and treatment options for avian influenza outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro findings and computational screening, which may not fully reflect in vivo efficacy. The evidence is moderate as it does not include clinical trials or long-term effects.

使用共振识别模型分析甲型流感病毒

Analysis of Influenza A viruses using the resonant recognition model
作者： Irena Cosic, Drasko Cosic, Ivan Loncarevic, Irena Ćosić
摘要中文翻译禽流感(禽流感)和人流感均由甲型流感病毒引起,但它们在 宿主 适应性、传播 和严重程度方面存在显着差异。在这里,我们使用了共振识别模型 (RRM),这是一种利用量子物理、生物化学和数学知识的创新方法,能够分析蛋白质与其目标(包括其他蛋白质、DNA、RNA 或小分子)之间的相互作用。使用RRM模型,我们识别了不同类型流感病毒的不同特征,并发现三组流感病毒与宿主细胞受体的相互作用完全不同。这一发现可以解释为什么不同的流感病毒只能与特定的 宿主 相互作用,以及为什么不同物种之间,特别是鸟类和人类之间的交叉感染极低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在使用共振识别模型分析甲型流感病毒,以了解 宿主 适应性、传播 以及禽流感病毒和人类流感病毒之间的严重性差异。
设计与对象
该研究利用共振识别模型,这是一种结合量子物理、生物化学和数学的创新方法,用于分析蛋白质与 宿主 细胞受体的相互作用。
核心方法
应用共振识别模型来识别不同流感病毒的特征,并根据它们与 宿主 细胞受体的相互作用将它们分为三组。
主要结果
鉴定出三组不同的流感病毒,它们与 宿主 细胞受体具有不同的相互作用,这解释了物种特异性的 宿主 适应以及鸟类和人类之间的低交叉感染率。
解释与创新
这些发现为为什么不同的流感病毒仅与特定的 宿主 相互作用以及为什么跨物种感染(特别是鸟类和人类之间)的感染率极低提供了新颖的解释。
科研与公卫意义
结果增强了对 宿主-病毒在 禽流感 中相互作用的理解,通过阐明跨物种 传播 的障碍,可能为 监测 策略和大流行病防范提供信息。
局限与证据强度
尽管创新方法为理解 宿主 适应提供了理论框架,但由于该研究基于未经实验验证的计算模型,因此证据强度中等。

Analysis of Influenza A viruses using the resonant recognition model

Authors: Irena Cosic, Drasko Cosic, Ivan Loncarevic, Irena Ćosić
Original abstract (English)
Bird flu (Avian influenza) and human flu are both caused by Influenza A viruses, but they differ significantly in their host adaptation, transmission and severity. Here, we have used the Resonant Recognition Model (RRM), which is innovative approach using knowledge from quantum physics, biochemistry and mathematics, capable to analyse interactions between proteins and their targets, including other proteins, DNA, RNA, or small molecules. Using the RRM model we have identified different characteristics for different types of flu viruses, and we have found that there are three groups of flu viruses with completely different interactions with host cells receptors. Such finding can provide explanation why different flu viruses can interact only with specific hosts and why there is extremely low cross infection between different species, in particular between birds and humans.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to analyze Influenza A viruses using the Resonant Recognition Model to understand host adaptation, transmission, and severity differences between avian and human influenza viruses.
Design and population
The study utilized the Resonant Recognition Model, an innovative approach combining quantum physics, biochemistry, and mathematics to analyze protein interactions with host cell receptors.
Core methods
The Resonant Recognition Model was applied to identify characteristics of different flu viruses and classify them into three groups based on their interactions with host cell receptors.
Main results
Three distinct groups of flu viruses with different interactions with host cell receptors were identified, explaining species-specific host adaptation and low cross-infection rates between birds and humans.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide a novel explanation for why different flu viruses interact only with specific hosts and why cross-species infection, particularly between birds and humans, is extremely low.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results enhance understanding of host-virus interactions in avian influenza, potentially informing surveillance strategies and pandemic preparedness by elucidating barriers to cross-species transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as the study is based on computational modeling without experimental validation, though the innovative approach provides a theoretical framework for understanding host adaptation.

家禽传染病气溶胶传播:微生物学、发病机制和控制措施

Aerosol transmission of infectious diseases in poultry: microbiology, pathogenesis, and control measures
作者： Suresh Burlakoti, Anh Dang Trieu Do, Adnan Alrubaye
摘要中文翻译集约化家禽生产的扩大提高了生产率,但增加了疾病风险 传播。在禽舍中,传染病 传播 通过直接和间接途径发生,包括表面污染和 媒介 传播的 传播。通常,家禽疾病控制主要侧重于禽舍中的直接接触和管理相关做法,但越来越多的证据强调了气溶胶 传播 途径的重要性,这是一种病原体传播的间接途径,可能会损害家禽的生产性能。禽舍饲养密度高、通风不足和生物安全措施差会增加细菌、病毒和真菌病原体雾化 传播 的风险。该途径的效率取决于颗粒大小、湿度、温度和通风,这些因素会影响病原体的生存和暴露,并进一步受到环境压力源和动物呼吸系统内作用部位的影响。鉴于禽流感病毒、传染性支气管炎病毒、禽正鸟病毒1型和曲霉属等主要病原体通常通过气溶胶途径传播,家禽养殖户必须采取一系列缓解策略,以实现有效的疾病预防和提高产量。因此,本综述强调了气雾剂 传播 在家禽健康和管理中的作用,重点关注微生物方面、传播、总体影响和缓解策略。此外,本综述旨在将当前关于气溶胶特性的知识与 传播 动力学相结合,并提供对未来研究机会的见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
集约化家禽生产的扩大提高了生产力,但增加了疾病 传播 的风险,突出表明需要了解气溶胶 传播 作为病原体传播的间接途径。
设计与对象
本综述重点关注微生物方面、发病机制 以及与禽舍中气溶胶 传播 相关的缓解策略,其中饲养密度高、通风不足和生物安全性差会增加 传播 风险。
核心方法
该综述将当前关于气溶胶特征的知识与 传播 动力学相结合,检查了影响病原体存活和暴露的颗粒大小、湿度、温度和通风等因素。
主要结果
主要病原体有禽流感病毒、传染性支气管炎病毒、禽正鸟病毒1型、曲霉属等。通过气溶胶途径传播,其效率取决于环境条件和压力源。
解释与创新
作者强调,气雾剂 传播 是家禽疾病控制中的一种重要但未被充分重视的途径,需要有针对性的缓解策略来提高生产性能。
科研与公卫意义
采用针对气溶胶的缓解策略对于家禽养殖户预防疾病和提高产量至关重要,特别是针对主要病原体。
局限与证据强度
作为回顾,该研究综合了现有知识,但可能缺乏原始数据;然而,由于气溶胶动力学和病原体特异性 传播 数据的综合整合,证据强度中等。

Aerosol transmission of infectious diseases in poultry: microbiology, pathogenesis, and control measures

Authors: Suresh Burlakoti, Anh Dang Trieu Do, Adnan Alrubaye
Original abstract (English)
The expansion of intensive poultry production improves productivity but increases the risk of disease transmission. In poultry houses, infectious disease transmission occurs through direct and indirect routes, including surface contamination and vector-borne transmission. Typically, disease control in poultry mostly focuses on direct contact and management-related practices in poultry houses, but there is growing evidence highlighting the significance of the aerosol transmission route – an indirect route of pathogen spread that can impair production performance in poultry. High stocking density in poultry houses, inadequate ventilation, and poor biosecurity measures increase the risk of aerosolized transmission of bacterial, viral, and fungal pathogens. The efficiency of this route depends on particle size, humidity, temperature, and ventilation, which influence pathogen survival and exposure, and is further compounded by environmental stressors and the site of action within the animal’s respiratory system. Given that major pathogens, such as avian influenza virus, infectious bronchitis virus, avian orthoavulavirus 1, and Aspergillus spp., are usually transmitted via the aerosol route, it is essential for poultry farmers to adopt a range of mitigation strategies to achieve effective disease prevention and improve production output. As such, this review highlights the role of aerosol transmission in poultry health and management, focusing on microbiological aspects, pathogenesis, overall impact, and mitigation strategies. Also, this review aims to integrate current knowledge on aerosol characteristics with transmission dynamics and to provide insight into future research opportunities.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The expansion of intensive poultry production improves productivity but increases the risk of disease transmission, highlighting the need to understand aerosol transmission as an indirect route of pathogen spread.
Design and population
This review focuses on microbiological aspects, pathogenesis, and mitigation strategies related to aerosol transmission in poultry houses, where high stocking density, inadequate ventilation, and poor biosecurity increase transmission risk.
Core methods
The review integrates current knowledge on aerosol characteristics with transmission dynamics, examining factors such as particle size, humidity, temperature, and ventilation that influence pathogen survival and exposure.
Main results
Major pathogens like avian influenza virus, infectious bronchitis virus, avian orthoavulavirus 1, and Aspergillus spp. are transmitted via aerosol routes, with efficiency dependent on environmental conditions and stressors.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors emphasize that aerosol transmission is a significant but underappreciated route in poultry disease control, requiring targeted mitigation strategies to improve production performance.
Scientific and public-health significance
Adopting aerosol-specific mitigation strategies is essential for poultry farmers to prevent disease transmission and enhance production output, particularly for major pathogens.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a review, the study synthesizes existing knowledge but may lack primary data; however, the evidence strength is moderate due to the comprehensive integration of aerosol dynamics and pathogen-specific transmission data.

意大利南部一家医院对急性感染进行 22 个月的综合征多重 PCR 检测:病原体 流行病学、诊断适当性和阴性结果的成本

Twenty-Two Months of Syndromic Multiplex-PCR Testing for Acute Infections in a Southern Italian Hospital: Pathogen Epidemiology, Diagnostic Appropriateness and the Cost of Negative Results
作者： Daniela Chirizzi, Angela Spedicato, Gabriele Bianco, Laura Lupo, Ilaria Serafini, Daniele Pisanò, Maria Rita Orsi, Giovanni Moschettini, Angelo Sorge, Rosanna Bruno
摘要中文翻译综合征多重 PCR 检测板可以快速、全面地检测急性感染中的病原体和耐药性决定因素,但其不受限制的使用可能会产生很大比例的阴性结果,从而产生重大的经济和管理影响。我们回顾性分析了在 UOSD 微生物学和病毒学中心进行的所有 BioFire FilmArray 脑膜炎/脑炎 (ME)、上呼吸道 (RP2.1)、肺炎 (PN) 和胃肠道 (GI) 测定。“Vito Fazzi”,ASL 莱切(意大利普利亚),时间为 2024 年 7 月 1 日至 2026 年 4 月 30 日。实验室信息系统中识别出同一患者对同一组的重复测定结果,并在进行任何分析之前将其删除,以便分析的每个测定结果都对应于一个不同的诊断事件。在 5381 项测定中(中枢神经系统,n = 761;上呼吸道,n = 2601;下呼吸道,n = 399;胃肠道,n = 1620),每个目标都有 59.3% 呈阴性,适当性梯度陡峭:ME 组为 90.0%,胃肠组为 77.1%,而上呼吸道和胃肠道组为 44.2%。肺炎组为 26.6%。CNS 阳性主要是病毒(75%),其中以肠道病毒为主;肺炎链球菌是唯一一致的细菌病原体。人类鼻病毒/肠道病毒在呼吸道生态中占主导地位;甲型流感几乎完全是 H3 和 H1N1pdm09,但 8.1% 的甲型流感阳性标本是模棱两可或不可分型的,并且从未转介进行测序,这是新型/人畜共患(禽)流感的可避免的 监测 盲点。肺炎群体耐药标记(mecA/C-MREJ、CTX-M、KPC、NDM)聚集在肠杆菌科细菌合并感染中。胃肠道组以致泻性大肠杆菌致病型(主要是 EPEC 和 EAEC)和艰难梭菌毒素为主,其 77% 的阴性表明了第二个过度利用的流。我们主张脑脊液细胞增多门控、分层/反射呼吸算法、针对社区发病腹泻的胃肠道门控,以及对不可亚型甲型流感的强制反射测序。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估意大利南部一家医院急性感染综合征多重 PCR 检测的 流行病学、诊断适当性和成本影响,重点关注阴性结果的高比例及其经济影响。
设计与对象
对在 UOSD 微生物学和病毒学中心进行的所有 BioFire FilmArray 脑膜炎/脑炎 (ME)、上呼吸道 (RP2.1)、肺炎 (PN) 和胃肠道 (GI) 测定的回顾性分析。“Vito Fazzi”,ASL 莱切(意大利普利亚)于 2024 年 7 月 1 日至 2026 年 4 月 30 日期间进行,删除了重复测定以确保不同的诊断事件。
核心方法
该研究涉及分析四个综合征组(中枢神经系统、上呼吸道、下呼吸道、胃肠道)的 5381 项测定结果,以评估病原体检出率、诊断适当性和耐药标记物,重点是识别可避免的 监测 盲点,例如无法分型的甲型流感。
主要结果
在 5381 项测定中,每个目标的阴性率为 59.3%,阴性率因小组而异:ME 为 90.0%,GI 为 77.1%,上呼吸道为 44.2%,肺炎为 26.6%。中枢神经系统阳性主要是病毒(75%),以肠道病毒为首,肺炎链球菌是唯一一致的细菌病原体。甲型流感几乎全部是 H3 和 H1N1pdm09,但 8.1% 是模棱两可或不可分型的,并且未转介进行测序。
解释与创新
作者将高阴性率解释为过度使用某些组合的证据,特别是 ME 和 GI,并强调了不可亚型甲型流感的可避免的 监测 差距,这可能会错过新的或人畜共患的毒株。新颖之处在于跨多个综合征组的病原体分布和耐药模式的详细流行病学图谱。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明需要有针对性的诊断策略,例如用于中枢神经系统测试的脑脊液细胞增多门控、分层呼吸算法和不可亚型甲型流感的强制反射测序,以提高诊断的适当性和针对潜在人畜共患流感威胁的监测。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和单中心设置而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。然而,大样本量和全面的分析为当地诊断实践和 监测 协议提供了中等的证据强度。

Twenty-Two Months of Syndromic Multiplex-PCR Testing for Acute Infections in a Southern Italian Hospital: Pathogen Epidemiology, Diagnostic Appropriateness and the Cost of Negative Results

Authors: Daniela Chirizzi, Angela Spedicato, Gabriele Bianco, Laura Lupo, Ilaria Serafini, Daniele Pisanò, Maria Rita Orsi, Giovanni Moschettini, Angelo Sorge, Rosanna Bruno
Original abstract (English)
Syndromic multiplex-PCR panels deliver rapid, comprehensive detection of pathogens and resistance determinants in acute infections, but their unrestricted use can generate a high proportion of negative results with substantial economic and stewardship implications. We retrospectively analysed all BioFire FilmArray meningitis/encephalitis (ME), upper-respiratory (RP2.1), pneumonia (PN) and gastrointestinal (GI) determinations performed at the UOSD Microbiology and Virology of P.O. “Vito Fazzi”, ASL Lecce (Apulia, Italy) between 1 July 2024 and 30 April 2026. Repeat determinations from the same patient with the same panel were identified in the laboratory information system and removed before any analysis, so that each determination analysed corresponds to a distinct diagnostic episode. Across 5381 determinations (CNS, n = 761; upper respiratory, n = 2601; lower respiratory, n = 399; gastrointestinal, n = 1620), 59.3% were negative for every target, with a steep appropriateness gradient: 90.0% negativity for the ME panel and 77.1% for the gastrointestinal panel versus 44.2% for the upper-respiratory and 26.6% for the pneumonia panel. CNS positives were predominantly viral (75%), led by enterovirus; Streptococcus pneumoniae was the only consistent bacterial agent. Human rhinovirus/enterovirus dominated the respiratory ecology; influenza A was almost entirely H3 and H1N1pdm09, but 8.1% of influenza-A–positive specimens were equivocal or non-subtypeable and were never referred for sequencing, an avoidable surveillance blind spot for novel/zoonotic (avian) influenza. Pneumonia-panel resistance markers (mecA/C–MREJ, CTX-M, KPC, NDM) clustered in Enterobacterales co-infections. The gastrointestinal panel was dominated by diarrhoeagenic Escherichia coli pathotypes (chiefly EPEC and EAEC) and Clostridioides difficile toxin, its 77% negativity identifying a second over-utilised stream. We argue for CSF-pleocytosis gating, tiered/reflex respiratory algorithms, gastrointestinal-panel gating to community-onset diarrhoea, and mandatory reflex sequencing of unsubtypeable influenza A.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the epidemiology, diagnostic appropriateness, and cost implications of syndromic multiplex-PCR testing for acute infections in a Southern Italian hospital, focusing on the high proportion of negative results and their economic impact.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis of all BioFire FilmArray meningitis/encephalitis (ME), upper-respiratory (RP2.1), pneumonia (PN), and gastrointestinal (GI) determinations performed at the UOSD Microbiology and Virology of P.O. 'Vito Fazzi', ASL Lecce (Apulia, Italy) between 1 July 2024 and 30 April 2026, with repeat determinations removed to ensure distinct diagnostic episodes.
Core methods
The study involved analysing 5381 determinations across four syndromic panels (CNS, upper respiratory, lower respiratory, gastrointestinal) to assess pathogen detection rates, diagnostic appropriateness, and resistance markers, with a focus on identifying avoidable surveillance blind spots like unsubtypeable influenza A.
Main results
Across 5381 determinations, 59.3% were negative for every target, with negativity rates varying by panel: 90.0% for ME, 77.1% for GI, 44.2% for upper-respiratory, and 26.6% for pneumonia. CNS positives were predominantly viral (75%), led by enterovirus, and Streptococcus pneumoniae was the only consistent bacterial agent. Influenza A was almost entirely H3 and H1N1pdm09, but 8.1% were equivocal or non-subtypeable and not referred for sequencing.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the high negativity rates as evidence of over-utilization of certain panels, particularly ME and GI, and highlighted the avoidable surveillance gap for unsubtypeable influenza A, which could miss novel or zoonotic strains. The novelty lies in the detailed epidemiological mapping of pathogen distribution and resistance patterns across multiple syndromic panels.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for targeted diagnostic strategies, such as CSF-pleocytosis gating for CNS testing, tiered respiratory algorithms, and mandatory reflex sequencing of unsubtypeable influenza A, to improve diagnostic appropriateness and surveillance for potential zoonotic influenza threats.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and single-center setting, which may affect generalizability. However, the large sample size and comprehensive analysis provide moderate evidence strength for informing local diagnostic practices and surveillance protocols.

日益重要的呼吸道病毒及最新进展

Önem Kazanan Solunum Yolu Virüsleri ve Güncel Gelişmeler
作者： Egemen Özdemir, Esra Kazak
摘要中文翻译Solunum yolu virüsleri son yıllarda artan tanı olanakları,yaşlı ve immün sistemi baskılanmış birey sayısının artması,değişen iklim koşulları,insan nüfusundaki artış,ulaşımın kolaylaşması ve bazı virüslerin Pandemiye neden olabilme potansiyelleri nedeniyle giderek önem kazanmaktadır。对于呼吸系统病毒、流感病毒和 SARS-CoV-2 病毒,我们已采取了相应措施。病毒、腺病毒、病毒、病毒等病毒都可能导致病毒感染。Zoonotik kökenli virüslerin çeşitli mutasyonlarla insanda hastalık yapma özelliği kazanması ve salgınlara yol açmasını COVıD-19 ve 禽流感 H5N1’deneyimledik。Peki güncel tehditler ve gelişmeler neler?解决此问题的方法是通过以下方法来解决此问题: Yeni 抗病毒药物 ilaçlar,单克隆抗核剂ve aşı çalışmalarında durum ne?一般的 derlemeyle önem kazanan solunum yolu virüslerinden bahsettik ve bu virüsler hakkındaki güncel gelişmeleri paylaştık。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究指出,由于诊断能力的提高、人口老龄化、气候变化、人口增长、交通便利以及某些病毒的大流行潜力,呼吸道病毒的重要性日益增加。
设计与对象
这是一篇讨论呼吸道病毒的一般性评论文章,没有具体的研究设计、人群或数据收集方法。
核心方法
未指定具体方法,因为这是一篇叙述性综述,总结了有关呼吸道病毒及其发展的当前知识。
主要结果
该综述确定呼吸道合胞病毒、流感和 SARS-CoV-2 会引起肺炎,可能危及老年和免疫功能低下患者的生命,并指出鼻病毒、腺病毒和人类偏肺病毒的 诊断 数量不断增加。
解释与创新
作者解释说,人畜共患病病毒可以获得 突变 的能力,在人类中引起疾病​​并导致大流行,正如 COVID-19 和 禽流感 H5N1 所经历的那样,并强调了 疫苗 和 治疗 的有前景的发展。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,疫苗 和 治疗 在预防某些呼吸道病毒方面取得了有希望的进展,但没有提供具体细节。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告具体的局限性或提供证据强度的详细评估,因为这是没有主要数据的一般性审查。

Önem Kazanan Solunum Yolu Virüsleri ve Güncel Gelişmeler

Authors: Egemen Özdemir, Esra Kazak
Original abstract (English)
Solunum yolu virüsleri son yıllarda artan tanı olanakları, yaşlı ve immün sistemi baskılanmış birey sayısının artması, değişen iklim koşulları, insan nüfusundaki artış, ulaşımın kolaylaşması ve bazı virüslerin pandemiye neden olabilme potansiyelleri nedeniyle giderek önem kazanmaktadır. Bu virüslerden özellikle respiratuar sinsityal virüs, influenza ve SARS-CoV-2 zatürreye neden olabilmekte, yaşlı ve bağışıklık sistemi zayıf hastalarda yaşamı tehdit edebilmektedir. Bunların dışında rinovirüs, adenovirüs, insan metapnömovirüsü giderek artan sıklıkta tanımlanan diğer solunum yolu virüslerindendir. Zoonotik kökenli virüslerin çeşitli mutasyonlarla insanda hastalık yapma özelliği kazanması ve salgınlara yol açmasını COVİD-19 ve Avian İnfluenza H5N1’de deneyimledik. Peki güncel tehditler ve gelişmeler neler? Bazı solunum yolu virüslerinden korunmak için aşı çalışmalarında ve tedavide umut verici gelişmeler mevcuttur. Yeni antiviral ilaçlar, monoklonal antikorlar ve aşı çalışmalarında durum ne? Bu genel derlemeyle önem kazanan solunum yolu virüslerinden bahsettik ve bu virüsler hakkındaki güncel gelişmeleri paylaştık.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the growing importance of respiratory viruses due to increased diagnostic capabilities, aging populations, climate change, population growth, transportation ease, and pandemic potential of certain viruses.
Design and population
This is a general review article discussing respiratory viruses without specific study design, population, or data collection methods.
Core methods
The methods are not specified as this is a narrative review summarizing current knowledge about respiratory viruses and their developments.
Main results
The review identifies respiratory syncytial virus, influenza, and SARS-CoV-2 as causing pneumonia that can be life-threatening in elderly and immunocompromised patients, and notes the increasing diagnosis of rhinovirus, adenovirus, and human metapneumovirus.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that zoonotic viruses can acquire mutation capabilities to cause disease in humans and lead to pandemics, as experienced with COVID-19 and Avian Influenza H5N1, and highlight promising developments in vaccines and treatments.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform that promising developments in vaccines and treatments exist for protection against some respiratory viruses, though specific details are not provided.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or provide detailed assessment of evidence strength as this is a general review without primary data.
